### [1] [Orchestrating Black-Box Schema Converters: An Empirical Study of Automated, Quality-Ranked Conversion Across Heterogeneous Schema Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26180)
*Felix Neubauer,Giridhar Chinnikkaramadom Govindan,Jürgen Pleiss,Benjamin Uekermann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种模式转换编排方法，将不同模式语言（JSON Schema、XSD、SHACL等）之间的异构转换器视为有向图中的黑盒节点，通过自动发现、执行、排序和溯源报告路径来实现自动化转换。基于此方法实现的Schema Conversion Orchestrator在60个真实任务中成功处理了43个，并定位了现有转换器生态系统的具体缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统常需要在多种模式语言中维护同一数据模型（如JSON Schema用于Web API、XSD用于数据交换、SHACL用于知识图谱），但模式语言间的转换器难以寻找、分布零散、质量参差不齐且常造成信息丢失，导致模型演化的构造和维护负担沉重。因此需要研究如何将这些不完美、异构的转换器编排为自动化、可复现、质量可排序的转换流程。

Method: 将模式语言建模为节点，转换器（视为黑盒）建模为有向边，转换路径通过自动发现、执行、排序和完整逐步骤溯源报告实现；失败时尝试替代路径。基于此实现开源工具Schema Conversion Orchestrator，集成到MetaConfigurator中，在跨五种模式语言的60个真实世界模式转换任务上进行评估，并使用智能体辅助、人工审核的质量标注。

Result: 在60个转换任务中，编排方法为43个任务生成了可用结果；剩余的失败任务准确定位了现有转换器生态系统的具体缺陷所在。

Conclusion: 编排方法能有效利用现有异构转换器实现自动化模式转换，并揭示当前转换器生态的局限性；研究结果对工具构建者和转换质量评估具有启示意义。

Abstract: Modern software systems routinely need the same data model in several schema languages: a model may exist as JSON Schema for a web API, as XSD for data exchange, and as SHACL for a knowledge graph. Keeping these representations consistent as the model evolves is a recurring construction and maintenance burden, because converters between schema languages are hard to find, scattered across ecosystems, of uneven quality, and frequently lossy. We study, empirically, to what extent such imperfect, heterogeneous converters can be orchestrated into automated, reproducible, and quality-ranked conversions, and where the current converter landscape reaches its limits. Our approach models schema languages as nodes and converters, treated as black boxes, as directed edges, so that conversions become paths that are discovered, executed, ranked, and reported with full per-step provenance, with failures handled by trying alternatives. We realize it as the open-source Schema Conversion Orchestrator, integrate it into MetaConfigurator, and evaluate it on 60 conversion tasks built from real-world schemas across five schema languages, using agent-assisted, human-reviewed quality annotations. Orchestration surfaces a usable result for 43 of 60 tasks; the remaining failures localize concrete gaps in the converter landscape. We discuss implications for tool builders and for measuring conversion quality.

</details>


### [2] [Augmentation with Dilution: A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Human Contributor Ecosystems After AI Coding Agent Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26289)
*Weixing Zhang,Bowen Jiang,Anne Koziolek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模因果推断研究，发现AI编码代理的采用并未显著减少人类贡献者的绝对数量，但显著降低了人类贡献者密度，减少了新手贡献者的相对参与份额（下降3.7个百分点），同时增加了代码审查深度（提升5.3%），呈现出"增强伴随稀释"的模式——AI并未取代人类，而是系统性地重塑了开源生态的参与结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅将人类贡献者视为静态背景，忽视了AI编码代理对开源生态中人类参与者的影响。本文首次将人类贡献者生态系统作为因变量，系统研究AI代理采用前后人类参与者的数量、构成和行为变化。

Method: 采用交错双重差分（Staggered DiD）设计，使用Sun and Abraham估计量进行因果推断。数据集包含2023年1月至2026年5月间的11,097个GitHub仓库，覆盖AI代理采用前后的观测数据。

Result: 1) AI代理采用未显著改变人类贡献者绝对数量（ATT=0.014, p=0.224）；2) 人类贡献者密度显著下降（ATT=-0.019, p=0.002）；3) 新手贡献者相对参与份额下降3.7个百分点（ATT=-0.037, p<0.001）；4) 审查深度显著增加5.3%（ATT=+0.0168, p<0.001）；5) 效应因项目规模、编程语言和项目成熟度而系统性变化。

Conclusion: AI代理并未取代人类贡献者，而是呈现出"增强伴随稀释"的模式——AI将负担从代码生产阶段转移到代码审查阶段，系统性地重塑了开源生态系统的参与结构。人类在代码审查环节的作用反而得到强化。

Abstract: AI coding agents are penetrating open-source software development at an unprecedented pace, yet existing research predominantly treats human contributors as a static backdrop rather than as the subject of inquiry. This paper presents the first large-scale empirical study that takes the human contributor ecosystem as its dependent variable, examining how the number, composition, and behavior of human participants change following AI coding agent adoption in open-source projects. Using a staggered difference-in-differences design on a dataset of 11,097 GitHub repositories spanning January 2023 to May 2026, we provide causal evidence via the Sun and Abraham estimator. Our results show that AI agent adoption does not significantly change the absolute number of human contributors (ATT = 0.014, p = 0.224), but significantly reduces human contributor density (ATT = -0.019, p = 0.002), indicating that the relative share of human participation declines as AI-generated pull requests accumulate. The relative participation share of newcomers declines significantly by 3.7 percentage points (ATT = -0.037, p < 0.001), with the effect emerging immediately after adoption and remaining stable throughout the observation window. Review depth increases significantly by 5.3% (ATT = +0.0168, p < 0.001), indicating that AI agents shift burden from the code production stage to the review stage. Moderator analysis reveals that these effects vary systematically with project size, programming language, and project maturity. Together, these findings present a pattern of augmentation with dilution: AI agents are not displacing human contributors, but are systematically reshaping the participation structure of open-source ecosystems.

</details>


### [3] [Towards Safety-Aware Mutation Testing for Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26456)
*Donghwan Shin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出安全感知变异测试（SAMT），通过在ADS模块间消息中注入时间限界故障来模拟真实交互故障，并基于STPA等安全工程框架派生变异规则，以提供更系统化的测试充分性评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模拟测试缺乏系统性的停止准则，现有覆盖度指标无法捕获导致大多数ADS事故的组件交互故障，而传统变异测试直接移植到ADS各模块存在不足。

Method: 提出SAMT范式：①在ADS模块间消息中注入时间限界故障以模拟交互故障；②基于STPA等安全工程框架自上而下派生变异生成规则；③将系统思维嵌入变异测试流程。

Result: SAMT提供评估测试充分性的严格机制，支持自动化场景生成，并指导ADS修复。

Conclusion: SAMT通过系统安全工程驱动的变异测试范式，有望弥补现有ADS测试充分性评估的空白，但尚需解决若干关键开放挑战。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing is essential for ensuring the safety of Autonomous Driving Systems (ADS), yet the community lacks a systematic criterion for determining when we can safely stop additional test scenario generation. Existing coverage metrics typically focus on individual component reliability or treat the ADS as a black box, failing to capture certain component interactions that cause most ADS accidents. While traditional mutation testing provides a falsifiable measure of test adequacy, directly porting code- and deep learning model-level mutations to the corresponding modules of ADS is insufficient.
  In this vision paper, we propose a paradigm shift toward Safety-Aware Mutation Testing (SAMT). Unlike traditional mutation testing, which creates mutants (i.e., faulty versions of the software under test) by injecting artificial faults into individual components, SAMT systematically injects temporally bounded faults into the messages exchanged between ADS modules to simulate realistic interaction failures. To ensure these mutants represent genuine hazards, we propose deriving mutant generation rules directly from top-down safety engineering frameworks, such as System-Theoretic Process Analysis (STPA). By embedding systems thinking into the mutation testing pipeline, SAMT provides a rigorous mechanism for evaluating test adequacy, enabling automated scenario generation, and guiding ADS repair. We also outline critical open challenges.

</details>


### [4] [An Empirical Study of LLM-Generated Specifications for VeriFast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26490)
*Wen Fan,Minh Tran,Sanya Dod,Xin Hu,Marilyn Rego,Danning Xie,Jenna DiVincenzo,Lin Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了LLMs为分离逻辑验证器VeriFast生成规范的能力，在303个C函数上测试了8种提示方法、10个LLM和3种输入类型。结果显示LLM能较好保留功能行为（>91%），但验证成功率仅31.4%，94%的错误源于LLM对SL验证器领域知识的欠缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态验证工具（尤其是基于分离逻辑的验证器）需要大量人工编写复杂辅助规范，而现有LLM相关工作主要针对非SL验证器。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统评估LLM为SL验证器生成规范的能力。

Method: 在303个C函数上，使用8种提示方法、10个LLM和3种输入类型，分两阶段进行实验。通过定量和定性分析评估生成代码和规范的功能行为、可验证性和错误类型。

Result: LLM在源代码和规范中保留功能行为的成功率超过91%，但最终验证成功率仅31.4%。Gemini 2.5 Pro和提供形式化契约可获得更高成功率。94%的错误源于LLM在VeriFast等SL验证器领域知识上的失误。

Conclusion: LLM生成规范尚不能有效满足SL验证器的要求，主要瓶颈在于缺乏领域特定知识。研究结果为优化LLM生成SL验证器规范提供了指导方向。

Abstract: Static verification tools can assure industrial scale software, but require significant human labor to write specifications. This is particularly true of static verifiers based on separation logic (SL verifiers), which excel at verifying heapmanipulating programs, but require many complex auxiliary specifications to reason about heap structure. Recent work applies large language models (LLMs) to generate code, tests, and proofs, including specifications for verifiers, but mostly targeting non-SL verifiers. To address this gap, this paper thoroughly evaluates how well LLMs perform when prompted to generate specifications for verifying 303 C functions with the SL verifier VeriFast. We explored eight prompting approaches, ten LLMs, and three input types in two stages. Quantitative and qualitative analyses are used to assess the LLM-generated code and specifications for functional behavior, verifiability and errors. The results show that LLMs preserve functional behavior in source code and specifications (both over 91%), but achieve modest verification success (31.4%). Using Gemini 2.5 Pro and providing formal contracts lead to higher success rates in our setting. Moreover, most errors (94%) come from LLMs' mistakes in the domainspecific knowledge of SL verifiers such as VeriFast. These findings provide guidance for optimizing LLM-generated specifications for SL verifiers.

</details>


### [5] [Evaluation-Strategy Gap in Fault Diagnosis of Deep Learning Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26492)
*Sigma Jahan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 现有DL故障诊断技术在跨程序场景下性能显著下降（平衡准确率差距0.190），根源在于特征中的程序级结构；曲率特征对未见程序有效，而优化器和激活特征仅对已见程序有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DL程序训练失败诊断昂贵耗时，现有技术评估仅使用程序内交叉验证，不足以反映部署时面对未见程序的真实性能，需量化跨程序性能差距并诊断其原因。

Method: 构建DynFault语料库，包含38个真实DL程序的5,542条故障注入训练轨迹；对比程序内评估与留出完整程序的跨程序评估，分析性能差距；进一步研究曲率特征、优化器特征和激活特征在已见/未见程序上的表现差异。

Result: 跨程序评估与程序内评估的平衡准确率差距为0.190；差距源于特征中的程序级结构；曲率特征对未见程序的故障检测有效，优化器和激活特征仅对已见程序有帮助。

Conclusion: 现有DL故障诊断技术在跨程序泛化方面存在显著性能差距，曲率特征更具跨程序鲁棒性，未来需开发对未见程序更适用的诊断方法。

Abstract: Deep Learning (DL) programs can fail during training for many reasons, and diagnosing the cause is a costly and time-consuming maintenance task. Techniques for diagnosing such failures are commonly assessed using within-program cross-validation, which may be inadequate for deployment settings involving previously unseen programs. It is therefore necessary to assess how performance differs across these settings and to identify the causes of any performance gap in established fault diagnosis techniques for DL. We investigate this gap using DynFault, a corpus of 5,542 fault-injected training traces from 38 real-world DL programs. We found a gap of 0.190 in balanced accuracy for existing fault diagnosis techniques between within-program evaluation and holding out whole programs. We also found the gap comes from program-level structure in the features, which led us to examine two runtime feature sets, curvature features and optimizer features, and their behavior on unseen programs. We found that curvature features are useful for instability detection on unseen programs, while optimizer and activation features help only on programs seen during training.

</details>


### [6] [Same Scrutiny, More Time: Eye Tracking Insights into Reviewing LLM-Labelled Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26505)
*Ranim Khojah,Francisco Gomes de Oliveira Neto,Mazen Mohamad,Julian Frattini,Philipp Leitner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过Wizard-of-Oz实验发现，代码标注为LLM生成虽不影响审查彻底性，但会改变开发者的注意力分配和审查策略，提示需要重新审视AI代码审查政策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码仍存在错误和幻觉，需要仔细审查，但开发者实际如何对待标注为LLM生成的代码尚不清楚。

Method: 采用Wizard-of-Oz实验，通过眼动追踪和退出访谈收集行为数据和反馈，结合贝叶斯数据分析和定性分析。

Result: 审查彻底性未改变，但开发者在LLM标注的代码上注视时间更长；开发者会根据逻辑正确性等标准调整审查策略，并利用prompt引导审查。

Conclusion: 标注LLM生成代码会影响注意力分配和审查策略，但未提升审查彻底性；开发者意图与实际行为存在差距，需重新审视AI辅助开发政策。

Abstract: Modern software development increasingly involves the use of large language models (LLMs) to generate code. Despite their rapid advancement, LLMs remain prone to errors and hallucinations, emphasizing the importance of careful code inspection. However, in practice, developers' trust in LLM-generated code and their willingness to review it thoroughly may differ from these recommendations. How developers actually behave when reviewing LLM-generated code remains largely unexplored. In this study, we conduct a Wizard-of-Oz experiment to examine how software engineers behave when code is explicitly labeled as LLM-generated during a code review task. We collect both behavioral data and participant feedback through eye-tracking and exit interviews. Combining Bayesian data analysis with qualitative analysis, we found that while the thoroughness of code review did not change for participants, they spent more time fixating on LLM-labelled code, indicating that the label itself influences attention. Practitioners also adapted their review strategy for LLM-labelled code by assessing the code based on specific criteria (e.g., logical correctness), or using the prompt to guide their review. These findings inform LLM-based tool design on labelling while incorporating the prompt as a software artifact. Our study reveals a gap between reviewers' intentions and actual reviewing behaviour, highlighting the need for software companies to revisit their AI policies (particularly regarding LLM-assisted development) to better support developers in reviewing LLM-generated code.

</details>


### [7] [ConcoLixir: Reactive LLM Discovery Oracles for Python Concolic Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26545)
*Dong Chen,Chih-Duo Hong,Fang Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ConcoLixir将LLM作为发现预言机扩展到Python concolic执行中，在求解器失败时生成种子和具体输入，并在覆盖停滞时定位未覆盖代码。相比基准测试，平均行覆盖率提升8.6-17.0个百分点，评估成本仅1.63美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python程序在concolic测试中面临三个固有局限：库调用导致符号变量降级为具体值；正则表达式、校验和、解析器等语义操作难以求解；路径探索会在已覆盖路径上停滞。

Method: ConcoLixir是一种反应式LLM扩展，LLM作为发现预言机（非求解器或正确性预言机的替代），执行三个角色：1）生成初始种子；2）在求解器失败后提出具体输入；3）当覆盖率停滞时定位未覆盖代码。每个候选输入均经concolic执行，仅基于观察到的覆盖率和路径约束指导后续探索。

Result: 在合成、真实世界和库目标上，相比无LLM预言机的基准concolic测试器，平均行覆盖率分别提升8.6、15.1和17.0个百分点。增益在语义屏障和库边界附近最强，完整评估API费用仅1.63美元。

Conclusion: 有界LLM发现能力可以补充符号推理而无需替代它，是一种成本效益高的互补方案。

Abstract: Concolic testing combines concrete execution with symbolic constraint solving, but Python programs expose recurring limits. Library calls can cause symbolic variables to downgrade to concrete values. Regular expressions, checksums, parsers, and other semantic operations can be hard to solve, and exploration can plateau on already covered paths. We present ConcoLixir, a reactive LLM extension for Python concolic execution. The LLM acts as a discovery oracle, not a replacement for the solver or a correctness oracle. It generates initial seeds, proposes concrete inputs after solver failures, and targets uncovered code when coverage stalls. Each candidate is executed concolically, and only observed coverage and collected path constraints guide later exploration. Across synthetic, real-world, and library targets, ConcoLixir improves mean line coverage over the baseline concolic tester without an LLM oracle by 8.6, 15.1, and 17.0 percentage points. The gains are strongest near semantic barriers and library boundaries, and the full evaluation costs \$1.63 in API charges. These results show that bounded LLM discovery can complement symbolic reasoning without replacing it.

</details>


### [8] [Quantum Mutant Equivalence via Transpilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26604)
*José Campos,Andriy Miranskyy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Transpiler-Based Equivalence (TBE)方法，通过将原始和变异后的量子电路在相同配置下进行转译并比较生成的OpenQASM代码，来识别量子变异测试中的等价变异体。在348,299个存活变异体上评估，TBE识别出32.1%的等价变异体，达到100%精确率和82%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件中变异测试日益普及，但等价变异体问题尚未解决。最近一项研究生成超过70万个量子电路变异体，约半数存活于现有测试，无法判断这些存活变异体是反映测试薄弱还是语义等价。

Method: Transpiler-Based Equivalence (TBE)：将原始电路和变异后的量子电路在相同配置下进行转译，然后比较二者生成的OpenQASM代码是否一致。

Result: 在348,299个存活变异体（其中92,011个为等价变异体）上评估，TBE识别出29,536个（32.1%）等价变异体，达到100%精确率和82%准确率。

Conclusion: TBE是一种轻量级且高效的量子等价变异体识别方法，能有效解决量子软件变异测试中的等价变异体问题，避免无效测试开销和分数扭曲。

Abstract: Mutation testing evaluates test suite quality by introducing artificial faults (mutants) and checking whether tests detect (kill) them. A central challenge is the equivalent mutant problem: some mutants are syntactically different from the original program but semantically identical to it and therefore cannot be killed by any test. If left unidentified, such mutants waste testing effort and distort mutation scores. In quantum software, mutation testing is increasingly used, but the equivalent mutant problem remains unsolved. A recent study generated more than 700,000 quantum circuit mutants and found that roughly half survived the available tests, making it unclear whether these survivors reflect weak tests or semantic equivalence. We propose Transpiler-Based Equivalence (TBE), a lightweight approach that identifies equivalent quantum mutants by transpiling original and mutated circuits under the same configuration and comparing their resulting OpenQASM code. We evaluate TBE on 348,299 surviving mutants, 92,011 of which are equivalent; TBE identifies 29,536 of them (32.1%) as equivalent while achieving 100% precision and 82% accuracy.

</details>


### [9] [Knowledge-Based Pull Requests: A Trusted Workflow for Agent-Mediated Knowledge Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26721)
*Xinyu Zhang,Weiwei Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出知识驱动拉取请求（KPR）工作流，将外部贡献者的代码、测试和交互痕迹视为知识源而非默认合并候选，通过项目方内部可信代理在项目环境中重新生成代码，从而将“知识是否应纳入项目”与“特定实现是否应合并”两个决策分离，降低跨信任边界的软件协作成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拉取请求将外部代码直接作为合并候选，导致理解意图、协商范围和治理长期项目责任的高成本；AI编码代理虽降低了代码生成成本，但上述认知与治理瓶颈依然存在。

Method: KPR工作流包含：外部协作方提供本地代码、测试及清理后的代理交互轨迹作为知识源；代理将其提炼为人工确认的知识包，并转化为设计备忘录、风险清单、测试计划等评审形式；项目方自有内部可信代理在项目环境、工程惯例、测试和安全策略下重新生成候选代码。

Result: 贡献了KPR工作流、候选工件模式、成本核算视图、协作网关架构，并在7个已合并的公开PR上进行了最小受控模拟实验，验证了KPR包可从真实PR材料实例化，并在描述消融、差异消融和合成毒化补丁条件下经受压力测试。

Conclusion: KPR是一个可经验测试的工作流，其价值取决于可审计的提取、转换和项目端重新生成能否降低理解和重做高上下文外部变更的成本。

Abstract: AI coding agents are changing the bottleneck in software collaboration: code is increasingly cheap, while understanding intent, negotiating scope, and governing long-term project responsibility remain costly. This paper proposes \emph{Knowledge-Based Pull Requests} (KPR), a trusted workflow for agent-mediated software collaboration across trust boundaries, including open source, enterprise, vendor, contractor, and customer-driven settings. In KPR, an external collaborator's local code, tests, and cleaned agent interaction trace are treated as knowledge sources rather than as the default merge candidate. Agents distill these sources into a human-confirmed knowledge package and render it into reviewer-facing forms such as design memos, risk checklists, test plans, or implementation briefs. A project-owned inner trusted coding agent then regenerates candidate code inside the receiving project's environment under repository context, engineering conventions, tests, and security policy. KPR therefore separates two decisions that traditional pull requests often collapse: whether the knowledge should enter the project, and whether a particular implementation should be merged. We contribute the KPR workflow, a candidate artifact schema, a cost-accounting view, a collaboration gateway architecture, a minimal controlled simulation pilot over seven merged public pull requests, and an evaluation agenda. The pilot shows that KPR packages can be instantiated from real PR material and stress-tested under description ablation, diff ablation, and synthetic poisoned-patch conditions. We position KPR as an empirically testable workflow: its value depends on whether auditable extraction, transformation, and project-side regeneration reduce the cost of understanding and reworking high-context external changes.

</details>


### [10] [A Deterministic Control Plane for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26924)
*Padmaraj Madatha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLM编码工具（如Cline、Copilot）的智能体配置层缺乏管理，表现为大量重复配置跨仓库传播（10.1%路径哈希重复，75.5%跨组织）、极少修改（58%单次提交）且几乎不声明权限边界（<1%）。提出Rel(AI)Build，一个确定性控制平面，通过SHA-256内容寻址、HMAC锁文件、哈希链审计日志、分层权限、攻击阻断列表、阶段状态机、多IDE编译和Jaccard相似性检测提示漂移等机制来管理智能体配置供应链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM编码工具赋予智能体广泛的文件和shell访问权限，但其配置层（规则文件、智能体定义、IDE特定markdown）缺乏管理，存在安全风险和治理盲区。

Method: 对大样本（n=6,145个智能体配置文件）进行实证分析，发现配置传播、修改频率和权限声明的三种模式；提出Rel(AI)Build确定性控制平面，包含内容寻址、锁文件、审计日志、分层权限、攻击阻断、状态机、多IDE编译和提示漂移检测等机制。

Result: 实证发现：10.1%的跟踪路径是SHA-256精确重复（跨组织75.5%）；58%单次提交；<1%声明权限边界。对注入违规的一致性测试确认每个机制执行其声明的不变量。

Conclusion: 智能体配置层必须采用确定性的、工具无关的治理方式，而非委托给进一步的LLM编排。

Abstract: LLM coding harnesses grant agents broad file and shell access, yet the configuration layer that steers them -- rules files, agent definitions, IDE-specific markdown -- is largely unmanaged. A prevalence study of 10,008 public GitHub repositories (n=6,145 agent config files) finds that agent configurations propagate as undeclared shared components: 10.1% of tracked paths are SHA-256 exact duplicates across independent repositories (fork-adjusted, threshold-independent), with 75.5% of clone pairs crossing organisational boundaries. Two further patterns are indicative: configurations are rarely revised (58% single-commit; 0.4 vs 0.6 commits/month age-normalised against CI/CD workflows), and rarely declare permission boundaries (<1% of agent configs vs 33% of Actions workflows, n=31 true positives).
  We propose a deterministic control plane above the harness that maps one-to-one to these gaps. Rel(AI)Build treats agent definitions as a managed supply chain (SHA-256 content addressing, HMAC-stamped lockfiles, hash-chained audit logs); enforces tiered permissions and attack-derived blocklists before LLM invocation; gates feature work through a phase state machine with requirement-to-file-to-test traceability; compiles a single canonical definition to seven IDE targets; and detects prompt drift via Jaccard similarity. Conformance tests on injected violations confirm each mechanism enforces its stated invariant; developer outcomes remain future work. Governance of this layer must be deterministic and tool-agnostic -- not delegated to further LLM orchestration.

</details>


### [11] [Are LLMs Ready for Anti-Pattern Detection in Microservice Architectures?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26927)
*Marco De Luca,Domenico Amalfitano,Porfirio Tramontana,Anna Rita Fasolino*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）通过基于提示的分析流程，在微服务架构中检测重复性架构反模式（APs）的能力。在包含16种反模式的基准数据集上，与静态分析工具MARS进行对比评估，发现LLMs在某些反模式上表现有竞争力，但在需要明确结构或跨服务依赖证据的反模式上存在明显局限，表明LLMs尚不能替代传统分析工具，但可作为有前景的补充手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统中反复出现的架构反模式会阻碍可维护性、可演化性和运行质量。现有的反模式检测方法多依赖静态分析和手工规则，存在工具依赖性强、检测逻辑有限、难以适应异构代码库等问题，因此需要探索更灵活、通用的检测方法。

Method: 采用基于提示的分析流程，利用三种通用LLMs对包含16种架构反模式标注的微服务仓库基准数据集进行检测，并与最先进的静态分析工具MARS在精确率和召回率指标上进行统一协议下的对比评估。

Result: LLMs在多个反模式上取得了有竞争力的性能，特别是在证据局部化、异构或语义丰富的反模式上表现良好；但在需要明确结构或跨服务依赖证据的反模式上，静态分析工具仍更为可靠。

Conclusion: LLMs尚不能完全替代传统静态分析工具，但已可作为架构评估中一个有前景的补充手段。

Abstract: Microservice systems are prone to recurrent architectural anti-patterns (APs) that hinder maintainability, evolvability, and operational quality. Most existing AP detection approaches rely on static analysis and handcrafted rules, which can be effective but are often tool-dependent, limited to explicitly encoded detection logic, and difficult to adapt to heterogeneous repositories. In this paper, we investigate whether large language models (LLMs) are ready to support architectural anti-pattern detection in microservice architectures through a prompt-based analysis pipeline over static repository artifacts. We evaluate three general-purpose LLMs on a curated benchmark of microservice repositories annotated with 16 architectural anti-patterns, and compare their performance against the state-of-the-art static-analysis tool MARS using a uniform evaluation protocol based on precision and recall. Our results show that LLMs can provide useful support for anti-pattern detection, achieving competitive performance on several anti-patterns, especially when the relevant evidence is local, heterogeneous, or semantically rich. At the same time, they exhibit clear limitations on anti-patterns that require explicit structural or cross-service dependency evidence, where static analysis remains more reliable. These findings suggest that LLMs are not yet a replacement for traditional analyzers, but already represent a promising complementary aid for architectural assessment in microservice systems.

</details>


### [12] [CLIR: Liveness-Driven and Structure-Aware Fuzzing for the Cranelift Compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26977)
*Shangtong Cao,Tianlei Song,Qiuping Yi,Tianyu Chen,Guoai Xu,Ningyu He,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CLIR，一个针对Cranelift编译器的差分测试框架，通过语法保持的分层生成策略、活跃度引导的指令精炼机制和诊断引导的跨架构适配方案，在72小时内发现24个跨架构bug，性能显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管编译器测试已发展数十年，Cranelift仍面临独特挑战：(1) 严格SSA形式下构造有效IR的困难，(2) 生成足够计算密度的指令序列以压力测试后端组件，(3) 在异构架构间平衡广泛后端覆盖与高效根因分析。

Method: CLIR提出三个核心机制：(1) 语法保持的分层生成策略（保证SSA有效性），(2) 活跃度引导的指令精炼机制（最大化计算密度），(3) 诊断引导的跨架构适配方案（促进高效根因分析）。

Result: CLIR检测到比cranelift-fuzzgen、wasm-smith、WASMaker分别多8倍、24倍、8倍的独特bug（RustSmith未发现任何bug）；72小时内发现24个跨架构bug，其中21个已确认、9个已修复。

Conclusion: CLIR作为一种集成多层次策略的差分测试框架，在Cranelift编译器测试中显著优于现有基线方法，验证了其核心机制在解决SSA有效性、计算密度和跨架构分析三大挑战上的有效性。

Abstract: Modern compilers are complex software systems that must correctly translate high-level programming languages into machine code across multiple architectures. Cranelift, a fast and modern compiler backend originally developed for WebAssembly and recently adopted as an experimental backend for Rust, has gained increasing importance due to its superior compilation speed compared to LLVM and comprehensive multi-architecture support, including x86-64, AArch64, s390x, and RISCV64. However, despite decades of development in compiler testing, testing Cranelift still presents unique challenges, including (1) constructing valid IR under the strict enforcement of SSA form, (2) generating sequences with sufficient computational density to stress backend components, and (3) balancing broad backend coverage with efficient root cause analysis across heterogeneous architectures.
  To address these challenges, we propose CLIR, a differential testing framework that integrates a syntax-preserving hierarchical generation strategy to guarantee SSA validity, a liveness-guided instruction refinement mechanism to maximize computational density, and a diagnosis-guided cross-architecture adaptation scheme to facilitate efficient root cause analysis across heterogeneous backends. Our comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that CLIR significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines, detecting 8x, 24x, and 8x more unique bugs than cranelift-fuzzgen, wasm-smith, and WASMaker, respectively, while RustSmith uncovered no bugs. Consequently, within 72 hours of testing, CLIR discovered 24 bugs spanning all target architectures, with 21 confirmed and 9 fixed.

</details>


### [13] [To Run or Not to Run: Analyzing the Cost-Effectiveness of Code Execution in LLM-Based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26978)
*Zhihao Lin,Junhua Zhu,Mingyi Zhou,Xin Wang,Zhensu Sun,Renyu Yang,David Lo,Li Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过两阶段实证研究分析了LLM修复智能体中代码执行的效益。研究发现：执行使用广泛但行为差异大；限制执行对修复成功率影响极小（仅下降1.25个百分点），但能大幅节省成本；执行效益高度集中而非均匀分布。结论认为执行应被视为有明确成本收益权衡的资源，而非默认能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM修复智能体普遍采用"生成-运行-修订"的迭代执行范式，但执行测试耗时且昂贵，其对修复效果的实际影响尚未被充分探索。本文旨在系统量化执行行为对修复成功率和成本的影响。

Method: 两阶段实证研究：第一阶段分析SWE-bench排行榜7,745条智能体轨迹以刻画执行行为模式；第二阶段在200个SWE-bench实例上对三种智能体（Claude Code、Codex、OpenCode）评估3,000次端到端修复尝试，比较四种执行范式的性能与成本差异。

Result: (1) 所有智能体和模型均使用执行，平均每任务8.8次测试运行，频率范围2-19次，后期执行成功率显著高于早期；(2) 执行限制对修复成功率影响甚微，Prohibited与Unrestricted之间仅差1.25个百分点且不显著，但大幅节省token和耗时；(3) 执行效益高度集中而非均匀分布。

Conclusion: 当前智能体对执行的使用缺乏选择性，在收益低的实例上也付出了成本。执行应被视为具有显式成本效益权衡的资源，而非默认启用的能力。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for program repair are increasingly built on a "generate-run-revise" paradigm, iteratively executing tests to evaluate and refine patches. This execution-based approach has become standard practice in state-of-the-art systems. However, executions can be time-consuming and expensive, yet their impact on these agents remains underexplored. In this paper, we conduct a two-stage empirical study over execution behavior in LLM-based program repair. To characterize execution behavior at scale, we first analyze 7,745 agent traces from SWE-bench leaderboard submissions. Second, we evaluate 3,000 end-to-end repair attempts across 200 SWE-bench instances and three agents (Claude Code, Codex, and the open-source OpenCode) under four execution paradigms, which allows for a fine-grained comparison of performance and cost. Our analysis reveals three key observations: (1) Code execution is used across all agents and models analyzed, with an average of 8.8 test runs per task. Execution behavior varies substantially across agents and models, with frequency ranging from 2 to 19 per task, and late-stage executions consistently achieve higher success rates than early-stage ones. (2) Execution restrictions have little effect on repair success: on commercial agents with SOTA models the resolve-rate gap between Prohibited and Unrestricted is only 1.25 percentage points and not statistically significant, while Prohibited saves substantial token and wall-clock cost. (3) Execution benefit is concentrated rather than uniform. These patterns suggest that current agents apply execution indiscriminately, paying its cost on instances where it provides little benefit. Execution, therefore, should be treated as a resource with an explicit cost-benefit tradeoff, not a default capability.

</details>


### [14] [How Much Static Structure Do Code Agents Need? A Study of Deterministic Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26979)
*Zhihao Lin,Mingyi Zhou,Yizhuo Yang,Li Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究轻量级静态分析（如调用图、继承层次等结构信息）能否为LLM代码代理提供确定性锚点，使其导航更稳定、可复现。实验发现：静态结构通过约束随机探索而非提升智能来改进代理行为——提升了定位精度（Func@5 +2.2pp）、缩短了交互轨迹（-1.6轮）、降低了运行方差（约减半），同时提供了不同仓库规模下的最优注入策略建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的代码代理在仓库导航中依赖关键词搜索，忽视了调用图、继承层次、配置依赖等结构化关系，导致导航具有随机性且难以复现。现有方法缺乏对结构化信息的系统利用，亟需探索轻量级静态分析能否为代理提供确定性引导。

Method: 以OpenAI的Codex为强基线，系统注入不同粒度的结构化注释（调用图、继承层次等），测量对定位精度（Func@5）、轨迹行为（交互轮数）、运行稳定性（方差、链接跟随率）的影响。通过对比不同方向性（前向/反向/双向）和密度（轻量/稠密）的注释效果，识别"确定性锚定效应"。

Result: (1) 锚定有效：轻量调用/继承拓扑使Func@5提升2.2个百分点，交互轮数减少1.6轮；(2) 锚定对规模敏感：最优粒度和方向性取决于仓库特征，稠密语义收益递减，hub密集项目宜用反向链接；(3) 锚定提升稳定性：链接跟随率从0.15-0.18升至0.21-0.24，运行方差约减半，Pass@1提升3.4个百分点，代价是约10%的额外输入token。

Conclusion: 静态结构通过使代理导航更规范、可复现而非更"智能"来发挥作用。建议实践准则：中等项目默认轻量拓扑，大型仓库剪除前向边，隐性依赖场景保留稠密标签。

Abstract: LLM-based code agents navigate repositories through keyword search but miss the structural relationships, such as call graphs, inheritance hierarchies, and configuration dependencies, that define how software actually works. This makes agent navigation stochastic and difficult to reproduce across runs. We investigate whether lightweight static analysis can provide deterministic anchors for these agents: stable structural facts injected as plain-text comments that constrain probabilistic exploration and make navigation more predictable. Starting from a strong baseline, Codex from OpenAI, we systematically inject varying granularities of structural annotations and measure their effects on localization, trajectory behavior, and run-to-run stability. Our study identifies what we call the deterministic anchoring effect: static structure helps less by making agents "smarter" and more by making their navigation disciplined and reproducible. Three observations support this finding: (1) Anchoring works: lightweight call/inheritance topology improves function-level localization (+2.2pp Func@5) and shortens trajectories (-1.6 interaction rounds); (2) Anchoring is scale-sensitive: the optimal granularity and directionality depend on repository characteristics, where denser semantics show diminishing returns and hub-heavy projects benefit from inverse-only links that expose "who-calls-me" without forward edges; (3) Anchoring stabilizes: tags raise link-following rate from 0.15-0.18 to 0.21-0.24, roughly halve run-to-run variance, and improve single-run reliability (Pass@1 +3.4 pp) on medium-scale repositories, at the cost of roughly 10% more input tokens. These observations suggest practical guidelines: default to lightweight topology on medium projects, prune forward edges in large repositories, and reserve dense tags for implicit-dependency cases.

</details>


### [15] [Cleaning Logs for Downstream Tasks (Registered Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27000)
*Zahra G. Yazdi,Van-Hoang Le,Nyyti Saarimäki,Donghwan Shin,Domenico Bianculli,Lionel Briand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LogPurifier，一种基于日志模板间依赖关系、与下游任务无关的日志清洗方法，旨在识别并移除与系统功能行为无关的自由消息，从而提升模型推理和异常检测等下游任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件系统生成的日志常包含大量冗余和无关信息，这些信息会负面影响下游分析任务（如模型推理和异常检测）的性能。现有日志清洗方法缺乏任务无关性，需要针对不同任务进行适配。

Method: 提出LogPurifier，一种基于日志消息模板间依赖关系的任务无关日志清洗方法。该方法通过识别系统功能行为相关的日志模板依赖关系，过滤掉无关的自由消息（free-standing messages）。

Result: 论文计划通过控制实验设计进行实证评估，分析LogPurifier对模型推理和异常检测两个下游任务的效果和效率的影响。

Conclusion: LogPurifier通过任务无关的方式清洗日志数据，有望提升下游分析任务的性能，为日志数据预处理提供一种通用解决方案。

Abstract: Background: Software systems generate logs during execution to record critical events and runtime information for troubleshooting and monitoring. However, in practice, logs often contain significant amounts of redundant and irrelevant information, which can negatively impact the performance of downstream analysis tasks, such as model inference and anomaly detection. Objective: The objective of this study is to clean log data by identifying and removing free-standing messages -- messages that are not relevant to the execution behaviors of interest and are interleaved with messages capturing the system's functional behavior. Method: To address this objective, we propose LogPurifier, a task-agnostic log-cleaning approach based on dependency relationships between log message templates. The paper presents a plan for an empirical evaluation using a controlled experimental design to assess the impact of LogPurifier on the effectiveness and efficiency of two downstream tasks: model inference and anomaly detection.

</details>


### [16] [The Spec Growth Engine: Spec-Anchored, Code-Coupled, Drift-Enforced Architecture for AI-Assisted Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27045)
*Hartwig Grabowski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Spec Growth Engine轻量级框架，通过机器可读的规范图谱、Spine上下文组装器、垂直切片增长协议和漂移门控机制，解决AI编码代理中上下文爆炸和规范-代码静默漂移两大结构性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理虽然显著加速了实现速度，但引入了两个现有规范驱动方法未能充分解决的结构性失效模式：(1)上下文爆炸——代理必须一次性推理整个仓库，随着上下文窗口填满导致输出质量下降；(2)静默规范-代码漂移——代码不断演化而规范停滞，两者间的分歧在修复成本高昂之前不可见。

Method: (1)机器可读的规范图谱：节点携带明确的契约/设计分离；(2)Spine上下文组装器：将代理上下文限定到所有权路径范围内；(3)垂直切片增长协议：强制执行最难优先的构建顺序；(4)漂移门控：将规范-代码分歧作为阻塞合并条件。该设计综合了Parnas信息隐藏、C4架构、ADR、Walking Skeleton、Reflexion Models、Fitness Functions等成熟软件工程原则。

Result: 该框架是一个轻量级、与代码耦合、机器可强制执行的整体方案，避免了RUP或MDA等重量级框架的开销。

Conclusion: 通过将多个成熟的软件工程原则综合为代码耦合的轻量级机器强制执行框架，有效解决了AI编码代理在大型代码库场景下的上下文爆炸和规范代码漂移问题。

Abstract: AI coding agents dramatically accelerate implementation speed but introduce two structural failure modes that existing spec-driven approaches do not fully solve: (1) context explosion -- the agent must reason over an entire repository at once, degrading output quality as the context window fills; and (2) silent spec-code drift -- code evolves, the specification does not, and the divergence becomes invisible until it is costly to repair.
  We present the Spec Growth Engine, a lightweight framework that addresses both failure modes through a machine-readable spec graph whose nodes carry explicit contract/design separation, a Spine context assembler that scopes agent context to an ownership path, a vertical-slice growth protocol that enforces hardest-first ordering, and a drift gate that makes spec-code divergence a blocking merge condition.
  The design synthesises well-established software engineering principles (Parnas information hiding, C4, ADRs, Walking Skeleton, Reflexion Models, Fitness Functions) into a lean, code-coupled, machine-enforced whole -- without the overhead of heavy-weight frameworks such as RUP or MDA.

</details>


### [17] [ATGBuilder: Feature-Assisted Graph Learning for Activity Transition Graph Construction with Seed Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27080)
*Chenhui Cui,Zixiang Xian,Danyu Li,Tao Li,Rubing Huang,Dave Towey,Shikai Guo,Jiakun Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ATGBuilder，一种利用大语言模型和辅助任务的特征增强图学习方法，用于从种子监督数据中构建高质量的活动转移图（ATG），显著优于现有方法并提升GUI自动探索工具的导航效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ATG构建方法存在不足：静态分析可能遗漏可行转移或提取不可行转移，动态探索可能产生不完整的转移图。虽然基于学习的方法将ATG构建视为种子监督的链接预测任务，但对活动布局和控件触发信息的利用仍然有限。

Method: ATGBuilder包含两个关键创新：(1) 利用大语言模型（LLM）从布局文件中总结UI活动元数据，生成紧凑的文本功能摘要；(2) 显式地将控件触发信息建模为边属性，并在训练过程中引入辅助的控件属性重构目标。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，ATGBuilder显著优于当前最先进的方法。消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。通过提供更好的导航指导，ATGBuilder能够提升自动化GUI探索工具的性能。

Conclusion: ATGBuilder通过特征增强的图学习方法，有效提升了种子监督ATG构建的质量，并在实际应用中证明了其对GUI自动化探索工具的改进价值。

Abstract: Android applications are organized around activities that provide visual Graphical User Interface (GUI) containers that host the UI and handle user interaction events. Activity Transition Graphs (ATGs) have been widely used to model apps' GUI navigation. However, the construction of high-quality ATGs is challenging: ATGs based on static analysis may miss acceptable transitions and may extract infeasible ones; while dynamically explored ATGs can yield incomplete transitions. Recent learning-based approaches can treat ATG construction as a seed-supervised link-prediction task. However, the use of activity-layout and widget-trigger information for ATG construction remains limited. We propose ATGBuilder, a feature-assisted graph-learning approach for seed-supervised ATG construction. ATGBuilder uses a Large Language Model (LLM) to summarize UI activity metadata from layouts into compact textual functionality summaries. ATGBuilder explicitly models widget-trigger information into the edge attribute: It then uses an auxiliary widget-attribute reconstruction objective on this information during model training. ATGBuilder's performance was evaluated across a series of ablations on the frontmatter corpus, and an experiment on benchmark using manually-checked ground-truth ATGs. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that ATGBuilder significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. We further demonstrate its effectiveness by improving automated GUI exploration tools through better navigation guidance.

</details>


### [18] [On the Reproducibility of Quantum Software Defect Datasets: A Case Study of Bugs4Q](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27124)
*Haruto Ohto,Yuta Ishimoto,Shinsuke Matsumoto,Shinji Kusumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究复现了Zhu等人关于软件缺陷数据集可重复性衰退的发现，聚焦于量子软件缺陷数据集Bugs4Q。实验表明，随着Qiskit版本从v0.20.1升级至v2.3.1，Bugs4Q的可重复性从62.2%骤降至16.2%，其中93.6%的失败由依赖问题引起。与先前工作不同的是，多数崩溃无法仅通过调整依赖版本解决，而需修改源代码（如迁移导入路径和API调用）。基于此，研究者构建了Bugs4Q-Robust补丁版本，将可重复性恢复至78.4%，强调了量子软件生态中持续数据集维护的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhu等人已证明传统软件缺陷数据集（如Defects4J）随时间推移会出现可重复性衰退，但量子软件缺陷数据集是否同样存在此问题尚不明确。本研究旨在通过复现研究填补这一空白，验证相关发现是否推广至量子软件领域。

Method: 对Bugs4Q数据集中的37个缺陷工件在21个Qiskit核心库版本上进行77,700次量子程序执行，统计各版本下的可重复性比例；对不可重现的案例进行人工根因分析，分类失败类型；基于分析结果构建Bugs4Q-Robust补丁版本。

Result: Bugs4Q可重复性从Qiskit v0.20.1的62.2%下降至v2.3.1的16.2%；93.6%的失败由依赖问题引起；与先前工作不同，多数失败无法仅调整依赖版本解决，需修改源代码；Bugs4Q-Robust将可重复性恢复至78.4%。

Conclusion: 量子软件缺陷数据集同样面临可重复性衰退问题，且衰退更严重（需源代码级修复而非仅版本调整），强调了在快速演进的量子软件生态中持续维护数据集的重要性。

Abstract: The reproducibility of software defect datasets is essential for obtaining reliable and comparable research results. Zhu et al. have shown that defect datasets such as Defects4J suffer from reproduction failures (i.e., reported bugs become non-reproducible) as time passes since their creation. However, it remains unclear whether these findings generalize to quantum software defect datasets. We therefore conduct a replication study of the prior work using Bugs4Q, a widely used dataset of real-world bugs in quantum programs. Our analysis includes 77,700 quantum program executions of 37 Bugs4Q artifacts across 21 core-library versions. The experimental results showed that the reproducibility of Bugs4Q dropped from 62.2% on Qiskit v0.20.1 to 16.2% on v2.3.1, the latest version as of April 1, 2026. A manual inspection of the root causes further indicated that 93.6% of the failures were dependency-related. While these findings are consistent with those of the prior work, we also observed differences. In particular, most reproduction failures in Bugs4Q cannot be resolved merely by adjusting dependency versions; instead, they require source-code modifications such as migrating import paths and API invocations. Based on this observation, we curated Bugs4Q-Robust, a patched version of Bugs4Q to restore reproducibility. Bugs4Q-Robust increases reproducibility from 16.2% to 78.4% on Qiskit v2.3.1. Our findings highlight the importance of continuous dataset maintenance in the rapidly evolving quantum software ecosystem.

</details>


### [19] [Smaller Models, Unexpected Costs: Trade-offs in LLM Quantization for Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27205)
*Fernando Vallecillos-Ruiz,Giordano d'Aloisio,Max Hort,Luca Traini,Antinisca Di Marco,Leon Moonen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了LLM量化在自动程序修复任务中的效果，发现量化虽减少最多85%内存占用，但增加了推理时间和能耗，且量化前后模型修复的问题集重叠度低，48%的配置被严格支配，表明量化效果对模型架构和任务复杂度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅用基准分数概括量化效果，掩盖了模型行为变化和非功能性开销，缺乏对复杂软件工程任务（如自动程序修复）中量化影响的系统评估。

Method: 在6个代表性LLM上，针对2个APR基准（HumanEval-Java和Defects4J），评估13种量化配置（不同位宽、方法、量化目标组件），分析有效性、内存占用、推理时间和能耗等指标。

Result: 量化模型与原模型修复的问题集重叠度低但修复数量相当；内存减少可达85%，但推理时间和能耗增加；48%的配置在帕累托分析中被严格支配；量化效果对模型架构和任务复杂度敏感。

Conclusion: 不存在普适最优的量化方法，量化带来的修复有效性、内存与能效之间的权衡高度依赖于底层模型架构和任务复杂度。

Abstract: Language Models (LLMs) are powerful toolsand have been increasingly adopted for complex software engineering tasks. As the number of parameters increases, results can often be improved, but this also imposes substantialmemory requirements. While quantization effectively reduces thememory footprint, its overall impact is often summarized onlyby benchmark scores, which mask changes in model behaviorand non-functional overheads. In this work, we conduct anempirical evaluation of LLM quantization using AutomatedProgram Repair (APR), a complex task in software engineering.We analyze 13 quantization configurations spanning differentbit-widths, methods, and target components (weights and KVcache) across six representative LLMs, evaluated on two APRbenchmarks (HumanEval-Java and Defects4J).
  Our findings reveal that base and quantized models can provide different sets of repaired problems with little overlap, whileretaining a comparable number of repaired problems. Althoughquantization successfully reduces memory footprints by up to85%, it increases both inference time and energy consumption,which we attribute to suboptimal hardware utilization. OurPareto trade-off analysis shows that 48% of the configurationsevaluated are strictly dominated by alternatives. Rather thanidentifying a superior quantization method, our findings highlightthat the trade-offs between effectiveness, memory footprint,and energy efficiency are sensitive to the underlying modelarchitecture and the complexity of the task.

</details>


### [20] [Beyond Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27258)
*Daniel Jackson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文批判了面向对象编程的核心原则——按问题域个体划分功能，指出该原则导致了功能碎片化和混淆问题，并提出放弃面向对象、采用将问题域个体与功能模块解耦的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向对象编程的核心原则（按问题域个体划分功能）已主导软件开发五十余年，但其导致的碎片化和功能混淆问题难以解决，需要重新审视这一基本原则。

Method: 通过分析面向对象原则的根本缺陷，论证其必然导致功能碎片化和混淆，并提出解耦问题域个体与功能模块的替代方法。

Result: 指出面向对象的核心原则本身就是问题的根源，而非后续发展试图解决的表面问题。

Conclusion: 解决功能碎片化和混淆的根本途径是放弃面向对象，采用将问题域个体与功能模块解耦的新范式。

Abstract: A core principle of object orientation -- that the functionality of a system can be partitioned amongst objects that correspond to individuals in the problem domain -- has influenced how software has been specified, designed and implemented for more than fifty years. Later developments in software engineering sought to build on this principle. But in fact this partitioning is neither natural nor straightforward, and the problems that these later developments sought to mitigate -- the fragmentation and conflation of functionality -- were often, in fact, the inevitable consequences of this founding principle. An easier path to addressing these problems therefore starts by going back, abandoning object orientation, and replacing it with an alternative approach that decouples the individuals of the problem domain from the modules that partition functionality.

</details>

### [21] [\chisao{}: A GPU-Native Parallel Optimizer for Multimodal Black-Box Functions via Convergence-Anticonvergence Oscillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26164)
*Ira Wolfson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种GPU原生的多模态黑箱函数优化器χίσαω，通过收敛-反收敛振荡循环实现并行模式发现，在Simon Fraser大学优化基准测试的全部42个函数上达到100%的模式恢复率，相比传统方法最高获得39倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有寻找多模态黑箱函数所有模式的方法（如盆地跳跃、CMA-ES、多起点梯度下降）均顺序执行，无法利用现代GPU的大规模并行能力，在高维复杂多模态问题上性能受限。

Method: 提出χίσαω算法：① 同时运行整个样本批次，利用收敛-反收敛振荡循环逃离局部陷阱并冻结已确认的模式；② 达到真正峰值的样本被"冻结"保留，其余样本通过基于动量的反收敛和随机平滑梯度继续探索；③ 采用两种互补的自适应重采样策略（Repulse Monkey和Golden Rooster）维持种群多样性。

Result: 在Simon Fraser大学优化基准42个函数上（维度d∈{2,4,8,16,32,64}），χίσαω在CPU基线方法于d≥8时完全失效的最难多模态函数上实现100%模式恢复；在Michalewicz d=64上较盆地跳跃获得34倍加速，在Rotated Hyper-Ellipsoid d=64上获得39倍加速；在噪声标准差高达1.0的情况下仍保持100%模式检测可靠性。

Conclusion: χίσαω作为GPU原生的并行优化器，在模式恢复率和计算效率上显著超越传统顺序方法，且对似然噪声具有强鲁棒性，已作为开源Python包发布。

Abstract: Finding all modes of a multimodal black-box function is a fundamental challenge in optimization, Bayesian inference, and scientific computing. Existing approaches -- basin-hopping, CMA-ES, multistart gradient descent -- operate sequentially and cannot exploit the massive parallelism of modern GPU hardware. We introduce \chisao{} (\textbf{C}onvergence-\textbf{H}alt-\textbf{I}nvert-\textbf{S}tick-\textbf{A}nd-\textbf{O}scillate), a GPU-native population optimizer that runs an entire sample batch simultaneously and exploits a deliberate convergence-anticonvergence oscillation cycle to escape local traps while freezing confirmed modes. The structural move is asymmetric: samples that reach true peaks are frozen (``stuck'') and preserved, while the rest keep exploring via momentum-based anti-convergence and stochastically smoothed gradients. Adaptive reseeding via two complementary strategies (Repulse Monkey and Golden Rooster) maintains population diversity throughout. On all 42 functions of the Simon Fraser University optimization benchmark suite across dimensions $d \in \{2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64\}$, \chisao{} achieves \textbf{100\%} mode recovery where all CPU baselines collapse at $d \geq 8$ on the hardest multimodal functions, at up to \textbf{$34\times$} speedup over basin-hopping on functions where all methods succeed (Michalewicz $d=64$) and up to \textbf{$39\times$} on unimodal functions (Rotated Hyper-Ellipsoid $d=64$, pure GPU dividend). All benchmarks evaluate the objective by value alone -- gradients come from finite differences -- so the reported speedups are a derivative-free worst case. Under substantial likelihood noise ($σ_{\mathrm{noise}}$ up to 1.0), mode detection remains 100\% reliable. The algorithm is available as a standalone open-source Python package on PyPI.

</details>


### [22] [Zero-Shot Size Transfer for Neural ODEs on Sparse Random Graphs: Graphon Limits and Adjoint Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26662)
*Mingsong Yan,Zhida Wang,Sui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了图神经微分方程（GNDEs）的零样本尺寸迁移原理，并建立了严格的定量理论。通过将图论神经微分方程（Graphon-NDEs）定义为GNDEs的无限节点极限，证明了在稀疏随机图上GNDE解以$O((\alpha_n n)^{-1/2})$的速率收敛到Graphon-NDE解。同时分析了离散-优化（DTO）和优化-离散（OTD）两种训练方式的一致性，实验验证了理论速率和零样本迁移的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNDEs的局部、尺寸无关的滤波器特性暗示其可以在小图上训练后直接部署到更大的相似图上（零样本尺寸迁移），但缺乏严格的理论支撑。本文旨在建立这一原理的定量理论，证明GNDE在稀疏随机图上的收敛性以及不同训练范式的一致性。

Method: 1) 定义Graphon-NDE和伴随Graphon-NDE作为GNDE正演和伴随系统的无限节点极限，并证明其适定性。2) 在稀疏随机图（从图论采样）上，推导GNDE解到Graphon-NDE解的逐轨迹收敛率，以及伴随系统（隐藏状态和参数梯度）的一致时间收敛界。3) 在显式欧拉离散化下分析DTO和OTD两种训练方式，证明两者渐近一致并给出误差阶数。

Result: 1) GNDE解以$O((\alpha_n n)^{-1/2})$速率收敛到Graphon-NDE解（高概率，忽略对数因子）。2) 伴随系统中隐藏状态和局部参数梯度的误差分别为$O(1/M)$和$O(1/M^2)$（考虑稀疏性和对数因子）。3) 实验在HSBM和tent图论上验证了理论收敛速率，并在四类图论上展示了零样本迁移的成功部署。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上严格证明了GNDE的零样本尺寸迁移原理，建立了GNDE在稀疏随机图上收敛到Graphon-NDE的定量结果，并为DTO和OTD训练范式提供了理论一致性保证。实验支持理论发现，表明该原理可用于高效地将小图训练的GNDE部署到更大图上。

Abstract: Graph Neural Differential Equations (GNDEs) model continuous-time graph dynamics by parameterizing Neural ODE velocity fields with Graph Neural Networks. Their local, size-independent filters suggest a zero-shot size-transfer principle: train on a small graph and deploy on larger, similar graphs without retraining. We develop a quantitative theory for this principle on sparse random graphs sampled from graphons. We consider Graphon Neural Differential Equations (Graphon-NDEs) and adjoint Graphon-NDEs as the infinite-node limits of the forward and adjoint GNDE systems, and establish well-posedness. For an $n$-node random graph with sparsity parameter $α_n$, we prove trajectory-wise convergence of GNDE solutions to Graphon-NDE solutions at rate $O((α_n n)^{-1/2})$, up to logarithmic factors, with high probability. We also establish uniform-in-time convergence bounds for adjoint systems governing hidden-state and parameter gradients. We further study discretize-then-optimize (DTO) and optimize-then-discretize (OTD) training. Under explicit Euler discretization with $M$ steps, we show that DTO and OTD are asymptotically consistent, with hidden-state and local parameter-gradient discrepancies of orders $O(1/M)$ and $O(1/M^2)$, respectively, up to sparsity and logarithmic factors. Experiments on HSBM and tent graphons support the theoretical rates, while zero-shot transfer experiments across four graphon classes demonstrate accurate deployment of learned GNDEs on larger independently sampled graphs.

</details>


### [23] [Algorithmic Foundations of Deep Learning: Complexity-Theoretic Rates and a Characterization of Universal Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26705)
*Anastasis Kratsios,Simone Brugiapaglia,Bum Jun Kim,Gregory Cousins,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经网络不应仅被视为灵活基函数，而应作为计算模型。证明了如果函数可由初等门语言上的实值电路计算，则神经网络能以显式的深度、宽度和非零参数界限逼近它，且网络复杂度由算法复杂度而非正则性主导。进一步证明满足并行化条件的可定义神经网络模型是通用逼近器当且仅当包含非仿射非线性。该理论统一了连续函数、Besov类、全纯函数的逼近保证，并展示了神经网络可模拟牛顿-拉夫森求根、幂迭代等数值算法。通过k顶点图的最短路径计算展示了精度：编译热带动态规划电路所得神经网络仅需O(log(1/ε))个非零参数，相比Lipschitz逼近的O(ε^{-c k^2})实现了指数级改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上通过最优基展开方案研究前馈神经网络表达性，仅通过正则性捕捉复杂度，无法区分具有类似正则性但直观上简单与复杂的对象（如平方根函数与典型布朗路径）。作者希望将神经网络视为计算模型，以算法复杂度而非仅正则性来解释其表达能力。

Method: 提出将神经网络视为初等门语言上的实值电路模型；通过编译电路到神经网络建立显式的深度、宽度、非零参数界限与电路复杂度之间的关系；引入可定义神经网络模型的并行化条件，证明通用逼近的充要条件（非仿射非线性）。

Result: 获得了连续函数的通用逼近保证、Besov类的极小极大最优逼近保证、全纯函数的对数误差复杂度；展示了神经网络可模拟牛顿-拉夫森求根和幂迭代等数值算法；在k顶点图最短路径问题上，编译热带动态规划电路得到仅需O(log(1/ε))个非零参数的神经网络，比Lipschitz逼近的O(ε^{-c k^2})实现了指数级改进。

Conclusion: 神经网络复杂度不完全由正则性决定，算法复杂度（计算模型视角）同样重要。这一理论框架统一了多种函数空间的逼近结果，并能解释神经网络在非正则任务上的高效性。

Abstract: Feedforward neural network (NN) expressivity is typically studied by emulating optimal basis-expansion schemes. While powerful, this perspective is incomplete: it primarily captures complexity through regularity, and therefore does not distinguish intuitively simple and complicated objects with comparable regularity, such as the square-root function and a typical Brownian path.
  The guiding message is that neural networks should be viewed not only as flexible basis functions, but also as models of computation. If a function is computable by a real-valued circuit over a prescribed elementary gate language, then it can be computed to comparable accuracy by an NN with explicit depth, width, and non-zero-parameter bounds controlled by the depth, width, gate count, and gate structure. Thus, neural-network complexity is not governed by regularity alone, but also by algorithmic complexity. We then show that any definable NN model satisfying a natural parallelization condition, allowing possibly multivariate non-linearities such as attention or layer normalization, is a universal approximator if and only if it contains a non-affine nonlinearity.
  The scope of our theory is illustrated by deducing universal approximation guarantees for continuous functions, minimax-optimal approximation guarantees for Besov classes, logarithmic-error complexity for holomorphic functions, and by showing that NNs can emulate numerical algorithms such as Newton-Raphson root finding and power iteration without architecture-specific arguments. Its precision is illustrated by shortest-path computation on $k$-vertex graphs: compiling the tropical dynamic-programming circuit yields NNs with O(log(1/ε)) non-zero parameters, exponentially improving in 1/ε over the generic $O(ε^{-c k^2})$ Lipschitz-approximation scale, for a constant c>0.

</details>


### [24] [Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27354)
*Haina Jiang,Liam Wang,Peng-Chen Chen,Min Seop Kwak,Seungryong Kim,Brian Bell,Jeong Joon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出误差条件神经求解器(ENS)，通过将PDE残差场作为网络输入而非优化目标，让模型学习迭代校正策略，在保持高效的同时显著提升预测精度，尤其在病态系统和分布偏移下优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经代理模型将求解视为纯统计任务，训练后无法自我修正违反物理约束的错误且外推能力有限；而混合方法通过梯度下降/高斯-牛顿步优化PDE残差虽能提升物理一致性，但继承了经典优化器的高计算成本和稳定性问题，且本文理论证明在病态系统中最小化残差可能不是重构精度的可靠代理。

Method: ENS将PDE残差场作为网络直接输入（而非优化目标），使网络能读取自身误差的空间结构并学习迭代更新策略来逐步校正预测。该方法不依赖昂贵的残差梯度优化，而是通过学习到的校正策略实现高效迭代修正。

Result: 在四个PDE族上，ENS在大多数设置中达到最高预测精度，在湍流Kolmogorov流上增益达10倍；同时避免了混合方法的高计算成本。ENS的学习校正策略在分布偏移（零样本参数变化、跨方程迁移）下也能泛化，在病态系统中优势最大。

Conclusion: 数值最小化PDE残差在病态系统中是重构精度的不可靠代理；ENS通过将残差作为输入而非优化目标，实现了更准确、高效且可泛化的PDE求解，为神经求解器设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Neural surrogate models offer fast approximate mappings from PDE parameters to solutions, but they typically treat solving as a purely statistical task: once trained, they struggle to correct their own constraint violations and extrapolate beyond the training distribution. Recent hybrid methods promote physical correctness by targeting the PDE residual via gradient descent or Gauss--Newton steps, but inherit the compute cost and instability of the underlying classical optimizers. We show, theoretically and empirically, that numerically minimizing the PDE residual can be an unreliable proxy for reconstruction accuracy in ill-conditioned systems, explaining why these methods often do not make accurate predictions despite achieving low residuals. We propose error-conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS), built on a different principle: rather than an optimization target, the PDE residual field is passed as a direct input to the network at each iteration, enabling it to read the spatial structure of its own errors and learn an update policy to iteratively correct its predictions. Across four PDE families, ENS attains the highest prediction accuracy in the large majority of settings, with gains reaching $10\times$ on turbulent Kolmogorov flow, while avoiding the expensive compute cost of hybrid methods. ENS's learned correction policy generalizes under distribution shift, including zero-shot parameter changes and cross-equation transfer, where its relative advantage is largest in the ill-conditioned regimes where residual minimization is least reliable. Project website: https://neuralsolver.github.io/.

</details>

*Vijay Yadav,Madhu Priya,Manish Dev Shrimali,Prabhat K. Jaiswal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力机制的物理引导卷积神经网络作为代理模型，用于学习非线性偏微分方程描述的微观结构演化，以Cahn-Hilliard方程控制的相分离过程为例，验证了模型在长时间演化中的稳定性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值求解器计算成本高，而深度神经网络代理模型作为高效替代方案受到关注，本文旨在设计一种能准确预测微观结构时空演化的代理模型。

Method: 采用基于注意力机制的物理引导卷积神经网络作为代理模型，训练其预测Cahn-Hilliard方程描述的二元混合物相分离全过程。

Result: 模型在长时间演化中保持稳定准确，适用于临界与非临界混合物，保持混合物组成守恒；准确捕捉域尺寸增长，符合Lifshitz-Slyozov域生长定律。

Conclusion: 该框架有效建模具有守恒动力学的系统，并可推广至其他复杂动力系统。

Abstract: The spatiotemporal evolution of many physical, chemical, and biological systems is described by nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs). Recently, deep neural network-based surrogate models have gained increasing interest as efficient alternatives to computationally expensive traditional numerical solvers. In this work, we propose an attention-based, physics-guided convolutional neural network as a surrogate model to learn the microstructural evolution of such systems. We train the model to accurately predict the full time-evolution of phase separation in binary mixtures governed by the Cahn-Hilliard equation. We show that predictions from our trained surrogate model remain stable and accurate over long-time rollouts for both critical and off-critical mixtures and preserve the mixture composition throughout evolution. We also show that our model accurately captures the growth of domain size and is consistent with the Lifshitz-Slyozov domain-growth law. The prediction results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for modeling systems with conserved kinetics and can be extended to other complex dynamical systems.

</details>


### [26] [Implementation of reinforcement learning in chemical reaction networks: application to phototaxis as curiosity-driven exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26168)
*Ruyi Tang,Grégoire Sergeant-Perthuis,David Colliaux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从最小认知视角出发，将单细胞藻类的趋光性重新定义为一种主观的、信息驱动的感知-运动过程。通过将部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）与化学反应网络常微分方程（CRN-ODEs）相结合，构建了一个框架，其中细胞通过无记忆贝叶斯步骤更新内部状态。利用逆强化学习（IRL）从30条实验记录的衣藻轨迹中推断行为目标，模型成功复现了经验性的光对齐分布。研究揭示了游动-翻滚交替作为一种信息获取策略：翻滚使细胞重新定向以采样新的感知配置并解决传感器模糊性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统趋光性模型将单细胞藻类的行为简化为机械的刺激-响应游动-翻滚过程，忽略了生物体主动采样环境以减少感觉模糊性的能力。本文旨在从最小认知角度重新理解这一导航过程，探索细胞内生化网络如何支持自适应的信息寻求行为。

Method: 1）构建POMDP与CRN-ODEs的联合框架，将环境变量设为隐藏状态，细胞通过无记忆贝叶斯更新维持最小内部状态；2）设计生物物理观测过程（光感受）和化学可计算的信息增益多项式界限；3）使用逆强化学习（IRL）从30条衣藻实验轨迹中推断行为目标函数；4）以随机模拟算法（SSA）为基准进行对比验证。

Result: 模型成功复现了衣藻实验中的光对齐分布，表现与基于目标函数的SSA基准相当。游动-翻滚交替被证明是一种信息获取策略：翻滚行为帮助细胞重新定向以采样新的感知配置，从而解决传感器模糊性。

Conclusion: 单细胞藻类的趋光导航可被理解为一种主观的、信息驱动的感知-运动过程。细胞内生化网络能够支持自适应的信息寻求行为，游动-翻滚的交替模式并非简单的刺激-响应，而是主动降低感觉不确定性的策略。这为理解生物系统的最小认知机制提供了计算框架。

Abstract: Living systems navigate environments using noisy and incomplete sensory signals. In unicellular algae, phototaxis is often modeled as a mechanistic run--tumble process driven by stimulus--response rules. However, such descriptions overlook how organisms actively sample their environment to reduce sensory ambiguity. From a minimal cognition perspective, we reframe this navigation as a subjective, information-driven sensorimotor process. To this end, we propose a framework linking a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) with biochemical reaction dynamics. Environmental variables are hidden, while the cell updates a minimal internal state from each observation through a memoryless Bayesian step. These internal dynamics balance orienting toward light with exploratory reorientation and can be implemented through Chemical-Reaction-Network Ordinary Differential Equations (CRN--ODEs). Our model includes a biophysical observation process for photoreception and a chemically computable polynomial bound on information gain. Using Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) on 30 experimentally recorded Chlamydomonas trajectories, we infer the behavioral objective consistent with observed phototactic motion and benchmark the resulting dynamics with standard Stochastic Simulation Algorithm (SSA) baselines. Our model reproduces the empirical alignment-to-light distribution, comparable to objective SSA baselines on this dataset. Within this framework, run--tumble alternation emerges as an information-acquisition strategy: tumbling reorients the cell to sample new sensory configurations and resolve sensor ambiguity, demonstrating how intracellular biochemical networks can support adaptive information-seeking behavior in cellular navigation.

</details>


### [27] [Neural Architecture Search for Generative Adversarial Networks: A Comprehensive Review and Critical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26169)
*Abrar Alotaibi,Moataz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了神经架构搜索（NAS）在生成对抗网络（GAN）设计中的应用，分类比较了不同搜索策略、评估指标和性能表现，发现进化算法和梯度方法在特定场景下具有优势，并指出了现有方法的局限性及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAS作为自动化设计GAN架构的关键技术，能够克服手动设计的局限性，本文旨在系统梳理和比较现有NAS-GAN方法，为研究者提供指导。

Method: 采用综合性综述方法，基于搜索策略、评估指标和性能结果等标准对现有NAS-GAN技术进行分类和比较分析。

Result: 发现进化算法和梯度方法在特定上下文中表现优越；传统评估指标（IS、FID）之外需要更鲁棒的评估指标；多样化数据集对GAN性能评估至关重要。

Conclusion: NAS能有效提升GAN的性能、稳定性和效率，但现有方法仍存在局限性，未来需开发更有效的NAS方法以推动GAN领域发展。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has emerged as a pivotal technique in optimizing the design of Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), automating the search for effective architectures while addressing the challenges inherent in manual design. This paper provides a comprehensive review of NAS methods applied to GANs, categorizing and comparing various approaches based on criteria such as search strategies, evaluation metrics, and performance outcomes. The review highlights the benefits of NAS in improving GAN performance, stability, and efficiency, while also identifying limitations and areas for future research. Key findings include the superiority of evolutionary algorithms and gradient-based methods in certain contexts, the importance of robust evaluation metrics beyond traditional scores like Inception Score (IS) and Fréchet Inception Distance (FID), and the need for diverse datasets in assessing GAN performance. By presenting a structured comparison of existing NAS-GAN techniques, this paper aims to guide researchers in developing more effective NAS methods and advancing the field of GANs.

</details>


### [28] [KG-TRACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Mechanistic Grounding in Antimicrobial Resistance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26179)
*Naman Garg,Sarika Jain,Sourav Yadav,Bharat K. Bhargava,Ghanapriya Singh,Abhishek Srivastava,Parimal Kar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KG-TRACE是一个将WHO突变知识图谱作为结构化生物约束整合到神经基因组模型中的神经符号框架，在结核分枝杆菌耐药性预测中达到0.9760 AUROC，并通过生物接地比（BGR）指标实现92.5%的符号覆盖，为临床提供可验证的审计追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于WGS的AMR预测模型虽已达到高准确率，但缺乏将神经归因锚定于既定生物学通路的机制，导致临床信任度不足。

Method: 提出KG-TRACE框架，通过可学习的认知信任门动态融合基因组特征与RotatE知识图谱嵌入，引入生物接地比（BGR）指标量化神经归因与生物学知识的一致性，并对不确定预测发出实验室随访标记。

Result: 在CRyPTIC结核分枝杆菌数据集上，对异烟肼预测AUROC达0.9760；对异烟肼耐药预测实现92.5%的符号覆盖率；能有效识别MDR共现伪影。

Conclusion: 神经符号接地为临床提供了可验证的审计追踪，弥合了预测准确性与临床信任之间的鸿沟，其核心价值在于符号接地而非预测性能提升。

Abstract: While WGS-based AMR prediction has reached high accuracy, existing models lack a mechanism to ground neural attributions in established biological pathways. We present KG-TRACE, a novel neuro-symbolic framework that integrates the WHO mutation knowledge graph (KG) as a structured biological constraint on a neural genomic model. Unlike existing methods that learn statistical patterns in isolation, KG-TRACE fuses genomic features and RotatE-based KG embeddings through a learned epistemic trust gate, dynamically weighting neural evidence against symbolic biological knowledge.
  Evaluated on the CRyPTIC M. tuberculosis cohort, KG-TRACE achieves an AUROC of 0.9760 for isoniazid, achieving competitive accuracy while its primary value lies in symbolic grounding, not predictive uplift. More importantly, we introduce the Biological Grounding Ratio (BGR), a dataset-level metric that quantifies alignment between neural attributions and established biology. Our framework achieves a 92.5% symbolic coverage of isoniazid-resistant predictions and effectively identifies MDR co-occurrence artifacts by issuing laboratory follow-up flags for 'UNCERTAIN' cases. We demonstrate that neuro-symbolic grounding provides a verifiable audit trail for clinicians, bridging the gap between predictive accuracy and clinical trust.

</details>


### [29] [Necessary but Not Sufficient: Temperature Control and Reproducibility in LLM-as-Judge Safety Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26185)
*Hiroki Tamba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大语言模型作为评估器（LLM-as-judge）的采样温度设置存在两个关键问题：温度未显式设置时默认值为1.0导致结果高度不稳定；即使强制设置temperature=0（贪婪解码），约1-2/7的边界案例仍不可复现。Claude Opus 4.7/4.8已弃用温度参数使主要缓解措施失效。建议将评估器分歧度作为一等健康指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"将采样温度设为0即可使评估确定性"这一普遍假设，揭示安全评估管线中评估器（grader）输出的随机性可能导致下游部署决策的虚假噪声。

Method: 以日本AISI的开源安全评估框架aisev为测试基准，跨越两个API提供商、三个模型层级、五种采样配置（含temperature=0, top_k=1等），执行690次API调用分析评估器输出一致性。

Result: 温度未设置时默认值为1.0，边界案例在20次运行中通过/失败不一致率高达~50%；即使采用forced greedy decoding (temperature=0, top_k=1)，7个边界案例中仍有1-2个不可复现。

Conclusion: 当前评估管线存在结构性缺陷：基于单次运行结果报告通过/失败判决会将被量化噪声错误呈现为安全属性；建议将评估器分歧度作为与分数本身同等重要的健康指标。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge ("grader") components are now standard in evaluation harnesses, including safety evaluations where a pass/fail verdict may gate downstream deployment decisions. A widespread assumption is that setting the grader's sampling temperature to 0 makes grading deterministic. We test this assumption against a real safety-evaluation codebase (Japan AISI's open-source aisev) and show it fails on two levels. First, the harness invokes its grader without setting temperature or seed; the underlying provider silently applies its default of 1.0, so items near the decision boundary flip pass/fail across identical runs (per-item disagreement up to ~50% over 20 runs). Second, pinning temperature=0 reduces but does not eliminate flips: across 690 API calls spanning two providers, three model tiers, and five sampling configurations, 1-2 of 7 borderline items remain non-reproducible even under forced greedy decoding (top_k=1). Claude Opus 4.7/4.8 has since deprecated temperature entirely, rendering the primary mitigation inapplicable to newer model generations. These findings expose a structural gap: evaluation harnesses that report single-run verdicts without variance or grader-disagreement metrics can present noise as a safety property. We release a reproduction harness (690 calls, 7 conditions) and recommend that harnesses treat grader disagreement as a first-class health metric alongside the scores themselves.

</details>


### [30] [Clue-Guided Money Laundering Group Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26189)
*Boyang Wang,Jianing Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Clue2Group框架，通过线索驱动的渐进式扩展方法，从初始线索出发逐步恢复洗钱犯罪团伙结构，解决现有图异常检测方法与真实反洗钱调查流程不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法主要产生节点级风险警报，而全局团伙发现方法被动搜索整个网络，二者均与真实的AML调查流程不匹配——分析师通常从具体线索出发，逐步扩展调查以恢复犯罪团伙。

Method: 提出Clue2Group框架：(1) 构建紧凑的局部调查上下文以减少噪声并保留链状和环状洗钱结构；(2) 使用多语义局部时序GNN估计线索条件化的局部风险场；(3) 整合风险、结构和先验模式证据以恢复连贯的洗钱团伙。

Result: 在两个大规模AML基准数据集上，Clue2Group提供了实用的线索驱动分析框架，弥合了基于图的AML研究与真实调查工作流之间的差距。

Conclusion: Clue2Group是一种可行的线索驱动图分析方法，为将图异常检测从节点级告警提升为团伙级推理提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Money Laundering Group Discovery (MLGD) aims to identify hidden criminal groups and recover their complete structures in large-scale financial networks. Existing graph anomaly detection methods mainly produce node-level risk alerts, while global group discovery methods passively search for suspicious groups over the whole network. Both are mismatched with real Anti-money-laundering (AML) investigations, where analysts usually start from a concrete clue and gradually expand the investigation to recover the responsible group. To address this gap, we propose Clue-Guided Group Discovery (CGGD), where a laundering group is progressively recovered from an initial clue set through analyst interaction. We further propose Clue2Group, a framework that first constructs a compact local investigation context to reduce noise and preserve chain-like and cycle-like laundering structures. It then estimates a clue-conditioned local risk field with a multi-semantic local-temporal GNN, and finally integrates risk, structural, and prior-pattern evidence to recover a coherent laundering group. Experiments on two large-scale AML benchmarks show that Clue2Group provides a practical clue-driven analysis framework for AML investigations, offering a feasible step toward bridging the gap between graph-based AML research and real investigation workflows.

</details>


### [31] [Federated Hash Projected Latent Factor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26192)
*Jialan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FHPLF模型，通过二进制梯度矩阵、投影汉明距离和隐私增强上传策略，在联邦学习框架中实现高效、隐私保护的哈希学习，在四个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统哈希学习需将数据集中到中心服务器，违反数据隐私法规；联邦学习虽能保护隐私，但传输大规模实值梯度导致高通信开销和隐私风险；现有哈希学习方法在联邦场景下二值码表示能力有限，影响模型精度。

Method: 提出FHPLF模型，包含三个创新：(a)用二值梯度矩阵替代实值梯度矩阵，降低计算、存储和通信成本并增强隐私；(b)利用投影汉明距离建模相似性，捕捉单个二值位的重要性提升表示能力；(c)提出安全二值梯度重组与隐私增强上传（SBG-PEU）策略，降低传输过程中的用户交互泄露风险。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上的实验表明，FHPLF在准确率、效率和隐私保护之间取得了优越的平衡，一致优于最先进的哈希学习和联邦学习方法。

Conclusion: FHPLF通过将二值表示与联邦学习框架深度融合，有效解决了传统方法在隐私、效率和表示能力之间的矛盾，为隐私保护的分布式哈希学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Hash Learning (HL) is an efficient representation learning approach that maps real-valued data into compact binary representations. Traditional HL methods typically require users to upload personal data to a central server, which is incompatible with increasingly stringent data security regulations. Federated Learning (FL) provides a decentralized paradigm for learning globally optimal models without centralizing private data. However, most FL methods rely on transmitting large-scale real-valued gradient information, leading to high communication overhead and potential privacy risks. Integrating HL into FL is a promising solution. Nevertheless, existing HL methods suffer from limited representational capacity of binary codes, which may degrade model accuracy. To address this challenge, we propose a Federated Hash Projected Latent Factor (FHPLF) model. FHPLF introduces three key innovations: (a) replacing real-valued gradient matrices with binary gradient-like matrices, significantly reducing computation, storage, and communication costs while enhancing privacy protection; (b) leveraging Projected Hamming Distance for similarity modeling, which captures the importance of individual binary bits to improve representation capability; and (c) proposing a Secure Binary Gradient Reassembly and Privacy-Enhanced Upload (SBG-PEU) strategy to further reduce the risk of user interaction leakage during transmission. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that FHPLF consistently outperforms state-of-the-art HL and FL methods, achieving a favorable trade-off among accuracy, efficiency, and privacy preservation.

</details>


### [32] [Statistical and Structural Approaches to Algorithmic Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26200)
*Antonio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出现代机器学习系统已演变为影响人类机会的社会技术架构，但现有算法公平范式存在两个根本局限：依赖确定性点估计进行审计，以及将个体视为脱离结构背景的孤立实体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法日益决定经济和社会机会的获取，但模型优化预测准确性的同时可能系统性边缘化弱势群体，现有公平性缓解策略建立在脆弱的简化假设上，效果有限。

Method: 识别并解决当代公平范式的两个根本局限：（1）审计时依赖确定性点估计；（2）将个体视为缺乏结构背景的孤立实体。

Result: 指出早期缓解策略基于脆弱的简化假设，在复杂社会技术环境中效果有限。

Conclusion: 现有算法公平范式需要超越确定性点估计和个体主义视角，以应对社会技术系统中的结构性不平等。

Abstract: Modern machine learning systems have outgrown their origins as isolated predictive constructs, evolving into complex socio-technical architectures that actively mediate human opportunity. As algorithms increasingly determine access to economic and social opportunities, it has become widely recognized that these systems are deeply embedded with the structural inequalities and prejudices of their environments. The field of algorithmic fairness emerged in response to the growing recognition that models optimized for predictive accuracy can systematically disadvantage marginalized groups. Early mitigation strategies, however, rested on fragile simplifications that limited their effectiveness in complex socio-technical environments. This thesis identifies and addresses two fundamental limitations of contemporary fairness paradigms: the reliance on deterministic point estimates for auditing and the treatment of individuals as isolated entities devoid of structural context.

</details>


### [33] [Topology-Informed Neural Networks for Flood Detection in Optical and Synthetic Aperture Radar Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26204)
*Sophia Li,Max Zhao,Raghu G. Raj,Tianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了拓扑数据分析（TDA）在洪水检测中的应用，通过从SEN12-FLOOD数据集中提取拓扑描述符并将其融入神经网络，证明拓扑特征能独立携带有意义的洪水信号，并与现有网络互补，构建更鲁棒且可解释的洪水检测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的洪水检测模型（如ResNet-50+GRU、Vision Transformer）虽然性能优异，但作为黑盒模型难以解释其决策边界和学习特征，在遥感这类安全关键领域存在可解释性不足的问题。

Method: 使用SEN12-FLOOD公开数据集，从每张图像中提取拓扑特征（拓扑数据分析），将这些拓扑描述符独立或与现有神经网络（如ResNet-50）结合，构建洪水检测系统。

Result: 拓扑描述符能够独立携带有意义的洪水信号，并且与现有网络互补，提升了洪水检测的鲁棒性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 拓扑数据分析为洪水检测提供了一种可解释的数学框架，能够捕捉数据的全局结构特征，弥补深度学习黑盒模型的不足，是遥感领域中值得进一步探索的方向。

Abstract: Floods frequently impact regions around the world. Rapid and accurate flood detection is crucial for emergency response and timely mitigation of human and economic loss. The expanding availability of satellite data and advances in artificial intelligence have enhanced monitoring of environmental hazards, but many flood events remain challenging to detect because cloud cover obscures optical satellite imagery. Rambour et al. introduced the SEN12-FLOOD dataset and extracted per-image features using a ResNet-50 convolutional neural network backbone, then fed these features into a gated recurrent unit network to show that temporal information can substantially improve accuracy compared to single-image baselines. More recently, Chamatidis et al. showed that a vision transformer can achieve strong performance with popular convolutional architectures. However, these models typically function as opaque black boxes, making it difficult to interpret their decision boundaries, learned features, and internal reasoning, especially in safety-critical domains like remote sensing. In contrast, topological data analysis (TDA) provides a mathematically grounded framework for capturing global structural features of data. TDA has emerged as a powerful tool for analyzing complex imagery, especially imagery with geometrically interpretable structures, of which floods are a prime candidate. In this work, we systematically evaluate topological descriptors for flood detection using the open-source SEN12-FLOOD dataset. By extracting topological features from each image and incorporating them into neural networks, we demonstrate that topological descriptors carry meaningful flood signals independently and complement existing networks to yield more robust and interpretable flood detection systems.

</details>
