TL;DR: 本文挑战了时间序列预测中“更大模型带来更好精度”的主流观点，通过Ridge回归的实验表明，优化预处理策略（如上下文长度、局部归一化、正则化和数据增强）能以更低成本接近甚至超越大型模型（Transformer、MLP、CNN）的性能。研究发现最优回溯长度具有序列特异性且与预测步长呈非单调关系，局部归一化优于全局归一化，且不同序列间超参数共享程度存在差异。优化后的超参数还可作为数据诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列预测研究倾向于使用越来越大的架构（从专用Transformer到通用基础模型），认为模型容量是提升精度的关键。作者持相反观点，认为通过优化预处理步骤可以更低成本地缩小性能差距，从而挑战“规模至上”的研究趋势。

Method: 以Ridge回归作为测试平台（因其具有闭式解和可解释权重），在8个标准基准上系统搜索以下超参数：上下文长度、局部归一化（学习上下文尾部分数而非整体）、正则化强度和增强策略。通过网格搜索直接读取最优超参数，并分析数据集内部的序列间差异。

Result: (1) 最优回溯长度具有强序列特异性，与预测步长呈非单调关系，拟合幂律指数从ETTm2的+0.46到Exchange和Traffic的-0.19，挑战了“更长预测步长需要更长历史”的传统认知。(2) 局部归一化（学习上下文的尾部分数）几乎普遍优于全局归一化。(3) 同一数据集内的不同序列间超参数存在分歧，最优跨序列共享程度从完全共享到完全独立不等。优化后的Ridge模型在大多数数据集-预测步长组合上超越线性预测器，并在8个基准中的6个上超越Transformer、MLP和CNN基线。

Conclusion: 预处理优化能以远低于模型扩展的成本实现有竞争力的预测性能，表明当前研究过度关注模型规模而忽视了数据预处理的重要性。优化的超参数还可作为数据诊断工具，揭示大型模型隐式吸收的数据结构。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting research has been moving steadily toward larger architectures, from specialized transformers to general-purpose foundation models, on the assumption that capacity is what unlocks accuracy. We take the opposite position: most of the gap can be closed at far lower cost by tuning preprocessing rather than scaling models. We use Ridge regression as the testbed, since it has a closed-form solution and interpretable weights, which let the optimal hyperparameters be read off the search directly. We search over context length, local normalization, regularization, and augmentation on eight standard benchmarks and find three patterns. (1) Optimal lookback is strongly series-specific and often non-monotonic in forecast horizon, with fitted power-law exponents ranging from $+0.46$ on ETTm2 to $-0.19$ on Exchange and Traffic, challenging the convention that longer horizons need longer history. (2) Normalizing over a learned trailing fraction of the context, rather than its entirety, is almost universally preferred. (3) Series within the same dataset often disagree on hyperparameters; the optimal degree of cross-series sharing varies from fully shared to fully per-series. The resulting models beat prior linear forecasters on most dataset-horizon entries and exceed Transformer, MLP, and CNN baselines on six of eight benchmarks. The optimized hyperparameters also serve as a diagnostic on the data itself, revealing structures that larger models absorb silently into their learned parameters.

</details>


### [108] [Recovering Governing Equations from Solution Data: Identifiability Bounds for Linear and Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27285)
*Yang Pan,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对从多解观测中唯一且稳定识别控制ODE的理论条件缺失问题，引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，建立了从线性到非线性Lipschitz/Hölder连续向量场等广泛结构方程的辨识界，并基于该度量推导了度量熵估计和样本复杂度界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前从观测数据学习控制方程的科学机器学习领域，缺乏对真实ODE能否被唯一且稳定识别的理论条件分析，也缺少样本复杂度的定量研究。

Method: 引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，该度量捕捉所有允许初始条件下两个方程的最坏情况分离，从而编码辨识问题的极小极大结构；基于此度量推导度量熵估计并分析样本复杂度边界。

Result: 建立了从线性ODE到具有Lipschitz/Hölder连续向量场的非线性类等广泛结构方程的辨识界，给出了需要多少解观测才能可靠恢复控制方程的样本复杂度量化界限。

Conclusion: Hausdorff距离为比较微分方程提供了合适的度量框架，基于此可对ODE识别问题进行系统的理论分析，填补了该领域在辨识条件和样本复杂度方面的理论空白。

Abstract: Learning governing equations from observed solution data is a fundamental challenge in scientific machine learning \cite{bruntonDiscoveringGoverningEquations2016,kovachkiNeuralOperatorLearning2023,longPDENetLearningPDEs2018,rudyDatadrivenDiscoveryPartial2017,raonicConvolutionalNeuralOperators2023}, yet the theoretical conditions under which a ground-truth ODE can be uniquely and stably identified from multiple solution observations remain largely undeveloped, and no quantitative analysis of the sample complexity of such learning tasks exists in the literature. To address this gap, we introduce the Hausdorff distance on solution sets as the natural metric for comparing differential equations, since it captures the worst-case separation between two equations over all admissible initial conditions and thus encodes the minimax structure of the identification problem. We establish identifiability bounds for governing ODEs across a wide class of structure equations--ranging from linear ODEs to nonlinear classes with Lipschitz (Hölder)-continuous vector fields--characterizing precisely when two distinct equations can be distinguished from solution data. Using this metric, we derive metric entropy estimates for the relevant ODE classes and analyze sample complexity bounds, quantifying how many solution observations are needed to reliably recover the governing equation.

</details>


### [109] [Designing Reward Signals for Portable Query Generation: A Case Study in Industrial Semantic Job Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27291)
*Ping Liu,Qianqi Shen,Jianqiang Shen,Wenqiong Liu,Rajat Arora,Yunxiang Ren,Chunnan Yao,Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Wanjun Jiang,Andrii Soviak,Kevin Kao,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RLAIF的端到端框架，用于生成可移植的求职搜索查询。研究发现，训练成功的关键在于奖励塑形纪律，而非优化器选择。GRPO因对虚假奖励信号敏感而易受利用，而引入规则基础的奖励下限可显著提升质量（+0.147）。训练时奖励模型将性能增益夸大了2.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求职平台的低带宽查询接口难以捕捉候选档案的高维复杂性，现有方法生成的搜索查询无法在去除个人标识符的同时保留泛化性资质。

Method: 提出端到端RLAIF框架生成可移植搜索查询；通过实证实验隔离优化机制与结构化奖励工程的影响；引入基于规则的奖励下限以纠正对逐字复制的奖励分配。

Result: 无评论家优化器的性能主要由稳健的奖励塑形决定，算法选择影响不大；GRPO对虚假奖励信号敏感，易被利用；引入规则奖励下限后质量提升+0.147；训练时奖励模型将性能增益夸大了2.4倍。

Conclusion: 训练成功从根本上依赖于执行奖励塑形纪律，而非选择替代优化器。

Abstract: Job-search platforms rely on low-bandwidth query interfaces that often fail to capture the high-dimensional complexity of candidate profiles. We present an end-to-end RLAIF (Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback) framework to generate \emph{portable} job search queries, terms that abstract away seeker-specific identifiers while preserving generalizable qualifications. This task introduces a highly adversarial reward surface where policy optimization frequently exploits flaws in LLM-as-judge rubrics, resulting in degenerate verbatim-copying behaviors.
  We conducted comprehensive empirical experiments to isolate the impact of optimization mechanics against structured reward engineering. Our results demonstrate that for critic-free optimizers, performance is overwhelmingly dictated by robust reward shaping, rendering the specific choice of algorithm largely immaterial. While critic-free per-rollout baseline methods (RLOO and REINFORCE++) natively resist reward-hacking, the group-relative advantage normalization in GRPO appears uniquely sensitive to spurious reward signals, making it disproportionately susceptible to exploitation. We show that introducing a deterministic, rule-based reward floor to correct for rewards assigned to verbatim copying mitigates this failure mode, resulting in a substantial $+0.147$ quality improvement on a cross-family evaluation judge. Ultimately, we show that the training-time reward model inflates performance gains by $2.4\times$, confirming that the training success is fundamentally dependent on enforcing reward-shaping disciplines rather than selecting alternative optimizers.

</details>


### [110] [A Multi-Fidelity Convolutional Autoencoder-Transfer Learning Framework for Guided-Wave-Based Damage Diagnosis Using Large Simulated and Limited Experimental Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27304)
*Santosh Kapuria,Abhishek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多保真迁移学习框架，结合轻量级物理仿真、卷积自编码器深度特征学习、前馈神经网络和少量实验数据，实现了板状结构中损伤的高精度定位与定量评估。该框架在计算效率、预测准确性（R²>0.93定位，R²>0.99定量）及泛化能力上均表现优异，为导波结构健康监测的实际部署提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导波结构健康监测中，深度学习模型的实际部署受到两个关键瓶颈制约：一是标记实验数据稀缺，二是生成大规模高保真仿真数据集的计算成本高昂。现有方法难以在数据有限条件下实现高精度损伤诊断。

Method: (1) 利用一维时域谱元模型生成大规模低保真合成数据集；(2) 采用卷积自编码器进行深度特征学习；(3) 通过迁移学习将预训练模型适配到实验域，仅需少量标记数据；(4) 结合前馈神经网络实现损伤定位与定量评估。

Result: CAE迁移学习框架在损伤定位精度上显著优于CNN对应方案；损伤定位R²>0.93，损伤定量R²>0.99；在未见过的损伤场景中仍保持高预测精度，展现出优秀的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架将轻量物理仿真与深度迁移学习高效融合，为实际工程中GWSHM应用提供了一种准确、计算高效且可行的解决方案，有效克服了标记数据稀缺与仿真成本高的双重挑战。

Abstract: Guided wave-based structural health monitoring (GWSHM) with onboard transducers offers significant potential for the early diagnosis of damage in engineering structures. However, the practical deployment of deep learning models is often hindered by the limited availability of labelled experimental data and the high computational cost of generating large-scale high-fidelity simulation datasets. This study presents a multifidelity transfer learning framework that integrates lightweight physics-based simulations, convolutional autoencoder (CAE)-based deep feature learning, a feed-forward neural network, and limited experimental measurements for accurate damage localisation and sizing in plate-like structures instrumented with piezoelectric transducers. A computationally efficient one-dimensional time-domain spectral element model is employed to generate a large synthetic dataset for pretraining, while transfer learning adapts the model to experimental domains using only a small amount of labelled data. The CAE-based transfer learning framework significantly outperforms its CNN-based counterpart in damage localisation accuracy. The model achieves excellent predictive performance with $R^2$ scores exceeding 0.93 for damage localisation and 0.99 for damage sizing. Its generalisation capability is demonstrated on previously unseen data, showing high prediction accuracy for damage scenarios not represented during pretraining or fine-tuning. The results establish the proposed framework as an accurate, computationally efficient, and practically viable solution for real-world GWSHM applications.

</details>


### [111] [Blackwell Approachability and Gradient Equilibrium are Equivalent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27315)
*Brian W. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan,Ryan J. Tibshirani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了梯度均衡（GEQ）框架在算法意义上等价于Blackwell可逼近性，进而通过已知等价链表明GEQ与遗憾最小化、校准等经典在线学习框架等价，并建立了高效的归约转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GEQ作为新兴在线优化框架与遗憾最小化等经典框架的关系尚不明确，已有工作仅表明两者目标不可比，缺乏精确的理论定位。

Method: 通过构造GEQ预言机与Blackwell可逼近预言机之间的双向高效归约，利用已知的可逼近性、遗憾最小化与校准之间的等价关系建立GEQ与这些框架的等价性；同时分析了GEQ的充要条件及有约束/无约束决策集下的归约关系。

Result: GEQ与Blackwell可逼近性在算法上等价，进而等价于遗憾最小化和校准；归约是高效的，可将乐观性、强自适应性等精细保证从遗憾最小化迁移到GEQ；给出了GEQ的充要条件。

Conclusion: GEQ并非孤立框架，而是在线学习等价核心的一部分，为跨框架的技术迁移提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Gradient equilibrium (GEQ) is a recently introduced online optimization framework that generalizes first-order stationarity from offline optimization and abstracts problems like online conformal prediction. While GEQ has curious similarities with known online learning frameworks, namely regret minimization, prior work has shown that GEQ error and regret are incomparable objectives, leaving open a precise understanding of how GEQ fits into the broader online learning landscape. In this work, we show that GEQ is equivalent to Blackwell approachability in the algorithmic sense. That is, a Blackwell approachability problem can always be solved using queries to a black-box GEQ oracle, with no asymptotic loss in the oracle's error rate, and vice versa. Taken together with known equivalences between approachability, regret minimization, and calibration, these results imply that GEQ is equivalent to these frameworks, as well. Our reductions are efficient and can be used to transfer refined guarantees, such as optimism and strong adaptivity, from regret minimization to GEQ. Along the way, we also identify necessary and sufficient conditions for GEQ, and establish reductions between different notions of GEQ with unconstrained and constrained decision sets.

</details>


### [112] [Beyond the Hard Budget: Sparsity Regularizers for More Interpretable Top-k Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27321)
*Nathanaël Jacquier,Maria Vakalopoulou,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种与Top-k SAE架构兼容的稀疏正则化器（ℓ1惩罚和ℓ1/ℓ2比率惩罚），在保持重建质量的同时提升单义性，证明硬性架构稀疏性与软性稀疏正则化是互补的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k SAE虽然避免了ℓ1惩罚的缺点，但存在固定预算k（无法适应输入复杂度）和过拟合训练k值的问题，此前未与显式稀疏正则化器结合。

Method: 在Top-k选择前的激活上施加两种正则化：1) 对未选中单元的ℓ1惩罚；2) 尺度不变的ℓ1/ℓ2比率惩罚，两者仅作用于批次活跃单元（在批次内至少被Top-k选中一次的单元）。

Result: 在两个数据集、三个视觉基础模型及多种k值下，两种正则化器一致提升单义性且不损失重建质量。ℓ1/ℓ2惩罚进一步将信息集中到更少潜变量，使重建对推理时k值选择更鲁棒，并提升小预算线性探针性能。

Conclusion: 硬性架构稀疏性与软性稀疏正则化是互补而非互斥的。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a leading tool for interpreting the representations of vision foundation models, decomposing their polysemantic activations into a larger set of sparse, more monosemantic features. The Top-$k$ SAE, a now-standard variant, enforces sparsity architecturally through its activation function, retaining only the $k$ most active latents per input. Because it was designed precisely to avoid the $\ell_1$ penalty used by earlier SAEs and its known drawbacks, it has not been combined with an explicit sparsity regularizer, despite retaining limitations of its own, such as a budget $k$ that is fixed regardless of input complexity and a tendency to overfit to the training value of $k$. We introduce two sparsity regularizers compatible with the Top-$k$ architecture, both acting on the activations before the Top-$k$ selection: an $\ell_1$ penalty on the unselected (off-support) units, and a scale-invariant $\ell_1/\ell_2$-ratio penalty that concentrates the code onto fewer effective units. Both penalties are applied only to the batch-active units, those selected by the Top-$k$ operator at least once within the batch. Across two datasets, three vision foundation models, and a range of $k$, both regularizers consistently improve monosemanticity at no cost to reconstruction quality. The $\ell_1/\ell_2$ penalty further concentrates information into fewer latents, making reconstruction more robust to the inference-time choice of $k$ and improving small-budget linear probing. Our central finding is that hard architectural sparsity and soft sparsity regularization are complementary rather than mutually exclusive.

</details>


### [113] [Hallucination in World Models is Predictable and Preventable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27326)
*Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出世界模型中的幻觉本质上是数据覆盖问题，通过在MMBench2数据集（427小时、210任务）上训练350M参数世界模型，识别出三种幻觉模式（感知型、动作边缘化型、场景发散型），并开发了相应的检测信号和两种数据覆盖增强策略（覆盖感知采样和好奇心奖励驱动的数据收集），实现仅用50条真实轨迹即可将预训练模型微调到全新环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成式世界模型虽能生成越来越逼真的可控未来帧，但频繁出现幻觉：视觉上流畅的 rollout 偏离了真实动态。作者假设幻觉集中在状态-动作空间的低覆盖区域，而轻量级数据中心的信号既能检测幻觉也能指导缓解。

Method: 1) 构建MMBench2数据集（427小时、210任务），包含真实动作、奖励和实时模拟器；2) 训练350M参数世界模型；3) 识别三种幻觉模式：感知型、动作边缘化型、场景发散型；4) 开发三种预测信号用于检测模型失败位置；5) 提出覆盖感知采样（训练时）和基于好奇心奖励的目标数据收集（在线）两种策略。

Result: 覆盖感知采样和好奇心奖励驱动的微调策略有效弥合覆盖缺口，仅需50条真实环境轨迹即可将预训练世界模型适应到完全未见过的环境。

Conclusion: 世界模型中的幻觉本质上是数据覆盖问题，用于检测幻觉的信号同样可以用于缓解幻觉。

Abstract: Modern generative world models render increasingly realistic action-controllable futures, yet they frequently hallucinate: rollouts remain visually fluent while drifting from the ground-truth dynamics. We hypothesize that hallucination concentrates in low-coverage regions of the state-action space, where lightweight data-centric signals can both detect it and guide mitigation. To test this, we introduce MMBench2, a 427-hour, 210-task dataset for visual world modeling with ground-truth actions, rewards, and live simulators, and train a 350M-parameter world model on it. We identify three distinct hallucination modes: perceptual, action-marginalized, and scene-diverging -- each anchored to a different stage of the pipeline, and develop three signals that accurately predict where the model will fail. To close coverage gaps at training time, we develop a coverage-aware sampling technique; to close them online, our hallucination predictors serve as curiosity rewards for targeted data collection, yielding a data-efficient finetuning recipe that adapts the pretrained world model to entirely unseen environments with as few as 50 real environment trajectories. Overall, our findings reveal that hallucination in world models is inherently a data coverage issue, and that the same signals used to detect it can also be used for mitigation.
  An interactive web version of our paper is available at https://www.nicklashansen.com/mmbench2

</details>


### [114] [Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27361)
*Danyal Rehman,Charlie B. Tan,Yoshua Bengio,Avishek Joey Bose,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），一种基于自回归建模而非正规化流的新框架，用于高效采样分子系统的热力学平衡态。ArBG克服了流模型的表达性限制和计算成本问题，在多个基准测试中显著优于基于流的方法，尤其在大型肽系统上表现突出。同时引入1.32亿参数的Robin模型，在零样本能量误差上比先前最优方法降低60%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有玻尔兹曼生成器主要依赖正规化流（NFs），但离散时间流受严格可逆性约束导致表达性有限，连续时间流则面临似然计算成本高昂的问题。作者旨在脱离基于流的范式，提出更具表达性、可扩展性且支持推理时顺序干预的新框架。

Method: 提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），采用自回归建模框架替代传统的流模型，能够规避流模型的拓扑约束，支持顺序推理时干预，并利用在大型语言模型中有效的架构实现更好的可扩展性。

Result: ArBG在所有基准测试中均显著优于基于流的模型，尤其在10残基的Chignolin等大型肽系统上提升明显。基于ArBG框架训练的Robin模型（1.32亿参数）在8残基系统的零样本能量误差E-W$_2$上比先前最优方法降低超过60%。

Conclusion: 自回归玻尔兹曼生成器有效克服了流模型的局限性，在分子平衡态采样任务上实现了性能突破，特别是大规模可迁移模型Robin的提出进一步验证了该框架的潜力。

Abstract: Efficient sampling of molecular systems at thermodynamic equilibrium is a hallmark challenge in statistical physics. This challenge has driven the development of Boltzmann Generators (BGs), which allow rapid generation of uncorrelated equilibrium samples by combining a generative model with exact likelihoods and an importance sampling correction. However, modern BGs predominantly rely on normalizing flows (NFs), which either suffer from limited expressivity due to strict invertibility constraints (discrete time) or computationally expensive likelihoods (continuous time). In this paper, we propose Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators (ArBG) -- a novel autoregressive modelling framework -- that overcomes these limitations by departing from the flow-based BG paradigm. ArBG circumvents the topological constraints of flows and enables sequential inference-time interventions, while offering enhanced scalability by leveraging architectures effective in Large Language Models. We empirically demonstrate that ArBG leads to significant improvements over flow-based models across all benchmarks, but particularly in larger peptide systems such as the 10-residue Chignolin. Furthermore, we introduce Robin, a 132 million parameter transferable model trained with the ArBG framework which improves over the previous state-of-the-art, reducing the zero-shot energy error, E-W$_2$, on 8-residue systems by over 60$\%$. The code can be found at the following link: https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg.

</details>


### [115] [Reinforcement Learning without Ground-Truth Solutions can Improve LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27369)
*Yingyu Lin,Qiyue Gao,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Xunpeng Huang,Kun Zhou,Tongtong Liang,Zhewei Yao,Yi-An Ma,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RiVER框架，通过校准的连续奖励（无需真实答案）训练LLM，在基于分数的优化任务上提升模型性能，并能泛化到精确解基准上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法依赖真实答案分配奖励，限制了其在无真实解场景（如分数优化任务）中的应用。作者希望在不依赖真实答案的情况下，利用确定性执行反馈作为连续值监督信号来训练LLM。

Method: 提出RiVER框架，针对连续奖励中的两个关键问题——尺度主导（分数未校准导致策略更新扭曲）和频率主导（频繁采样的次优解压倒罕见强解），设计了校准奖励塑造方法：通过实例级比较进行归一化，强调排名靠前的求解器，同时为其他有效解保留有界反馈。

Result: 在12个AtCoder启发式竞赛任务上训练，在ALE-Bench上Qwen3-8B和GLM-Z1-9B-0414分别提升8.9%和9.4%的评级排名。在LiveCodeBench和USACO上分别平均提升2.4%和3.5%。

Conclusion: 基于分数的优化任务结合适当的奖励校准，可以在无需真实答案的情况下作为通用编码能力的有效训练环境，且优于直接使用原始执行分数的基线方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for training LLMs typically rely on ground-truth answers to assign rewards, limiting their applicability to tasks where the ground-truth solution is unknown. We introduce a \textbf{R}anking-\textbf{i}nduced \textbf{VER}ifiable framework (RiVER) that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks without ground-truth solutions, using deterministic execution feedback as continuous-valued supervision. When applying group-relative RL to such continuous rewards, we identify two key challenges: \emph{scale dominance}, where uncalibrated score magnitudes across test instances distort policy updates, and \emph{frequency dominance}, where repeatedly sampled suboptimal solutions can outweigh rare but stronger candidates. RiVER addresses these challenges with calibrated reward shaping that uses instance-wise comparisons and emphasizes top-ranked solvers while retaining bounded feedback for other valid solutions. We train on 12 AtCoder Heuristic Contest tasks and evaluate on Algorithm Engineering Benchmark (ALE-Bench), LiveCodeBench, and USACO. RiVER advances Qwen3-8B and GLM-Z1-9B-0414 by 8.9\% and 9.4\% in ALE rating rank. More importantly, despite training exclusively on score-based tasks without any ground-truth solutions, RiVER also improves the backbones across exact-solution benchmarks such as LiveCodeBench and USACO by an absolute average improvement of 2.4\% and 3.5\%. By contrast, baselines trained with raw execution scores improve ALE rating but fail to transfer to exact-solution benchmarks. These results suggest that score-based optimization tasks, combined with proper reward calibration, can serve as effective training environments for general coding ability without ground-truth solutions.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Reducing Redundancy in Whole-Slide Image Patching for Scalable Indexing and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26157)
*Jialiang Geng,Ghazal Alabtah,Saghir Alfasly,Wataru Uegami,H. R. Tizhoosh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ARReST是一种通过识别并剪除跨类别判别贡献低的冗余病理图像块来压缩全切片图像索引的方法，在TCGA数据集上实现3%-60%的存储压缩而不牺牲检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字病理学快速发展，全切片图像(WSI)索引与检索需求迫切，尤其是检索增强生成(RAG)等生成式AI工作流需要可靠的相似性搜索支持临床决策。然而高性能存储的高昂成本限制了WSI索引的可扩展性和可及性，亟需降低存储需求同时保持检索精度的方法。

Method: 提出ARReST（Antithetical Redundancy Reduction Strategy），一种利用跨不相似组织类别的冗余性的对立性框架。不同于仅消除类内重复，ARReST识别"对立性"图像块（即对跨类别判别贡献最小的表示）并将其从可搜索档案中剪除，从而在保持形态多样性和检索保真度的前提下压缩索引。

Result: 在包含21个器官的TCGA数据集上的实验表明，ARReST实现了显著的索引压缩，存储节省范围为3%至60%（平均14%±13%），且不损害检索性能。

Conclusion: ARReST实现了可扩展、成本高效的WSI索引，适用于下一代检索驱动的临床AI系统。

Abstract: The rapid growth of digital pathology has created an urgent need for efficient indexing and retrieval of whole slide images (WSIs). This need is intensified by emerging generative AI workflows, particularly retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), which require dependable similarity search to support high-stakes clinical decision-making. Yet the substantial cost of high-performance storage limits the scalability and accessibility of WSI indexing for many healthcare institutions. Consequently, methods that can reduce storage demands while preserving retrieval accuracy have become a critical research priority. We propose ARReST (Antithetical Redundancy Reduction Strategy), a principled oppositional framework that leverages redundancy across dissimilar tissue classes to markedly decrease the number of patches that must be indexed from each WSI. Instead of eliminating only within-class duplicates, ARReST identifies antithetical patches-those whose representations contribute minimally to cross-class discrimination-and prunes them from the searchable archive. This targeted reduction substantially compresses the index without sacrificing morphological diversity or retrieval fidelity. By minimizing superfluous patch representations, ARReST reduces storage footprint, lowers computational overhead, and accelerates similarity search across large pathology repositories. Extensive experiments on TCGA repository (The Cancer Genome Atlas with 21 organs) demonstrate that ARReST achieves significant index compression while maintaining competitive retrieval performance. The observed storage savings of 3% to 60% (14%$\pm$13%) can be reliably achieved without compromising retrieval performance for many organs. The proposed strategy enables scalable, cost-efficient WSI indexing and is well-suited for next-generation retrieval-driven clinical AI systems.

</details>


### [117] [From Clicks to Intent: Cross-Platform Session Embeddings with LLM-Distilled Taxonomy for Financial Services Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26277)
*Dianjing Fan,Yao Li,Kyaw Hpone Myint,Dwipam Katariya,Alexandre G. R. Day,Pranab Mohanty,Giri Iyengar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展的双重意图预测框架，利用自监督Transformer将网页点击流编码为紧凑会话嵌入，并结合LLM生成可解释意图标签，同时服务于定量推荐和定性分析任务。在移动端首页磁贴排序任务中，会话嵌入相比生产基线在Recall@1上提升1.88%，Log Loss降低13.38%；在用户转化预测任务中，嵌入相比LLM标签在micro F1上提升4.3%，而蒸馏层以极低延迟提供可解释标签，性能仅下降7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融服务中，登录前的网页交互与登录后的应用内体验存在巨大差异，跨渠道实体解析困难导致网页意图信号未被充分利用，现有方法缺乏灵活性，无法同时支持定量推荐与定性理解。

Method: 将原始网页点击流转换为两种输出：自监督Transformer编码多模态点击流为紧凑会话嵌入；基于LLM的类别生成与蒸馏流程生成可解释意图标签。

Result: 移动端首页磁贴排序任务：会话嵌入提升Recall@1 1.88%，降低Log Loss 13.38%。用户转化预测任务：嵌入比LLM标签micro F1高4.3%；蒸馏层以极低延迟提供可解释标签，性能仅下降7%。

Conclusion: 自监督点击流表示结合LLM蒸馏类别可同时服务定量任务与定性理解，在生产环境中有效。

Abstract: Sequential user behavior modeling is widely adopted in industrial recommender systems; however, significant gaps remain in financial services, where pre-login web interactions and authenticated in-app experiences differ drastically. Specifically, pre-login web users typically explore new products, whereas logged-in app users focus on account servicing. Due to the challenge of cross-channel entity resolution (e.g., matching anonymous web sessions to authenticated mobile accounts), web-based intent signals remain underutilized for post-authentication personalization. Existing methods for capturing web-based intent are often ad-hoc and narrow, lacking the flexibility to support both quantitative downstream recommendations and qualitative understanding at scale. In this work, we propose a scalable and dual-purpose intent prediction framework for web-based interactions and demonstrate its applicability for personalization. Our approach transforms raw web clickstreams into two outputs: a self-supervised Transformer encodes multi-modal clickstreams into a compact session embedding, while an LLM-based taxonomy generation and distillation pipeline produces interpretable intent labels. Our system demonstrates that self-supervised clickstream representations combined with LLM-distilled taxonomies can jointly serve quantitative tasks and qualitative understanding in production: on the mobile homepage tile ranking task, the session embedding improves macro Recall@1 by 1.88% and reduces Log Loss by 13.38% over production baselines. On the user conversion prediction task, the embedding outperforms the LLM labels by 4.3% on micro F1, while the distillation layer delivers interpretable labels at ultra-low latency with only a 7% performance drop.

</details>


### [118] [Scoring Is Not Enough: Addressing Gaps in Utility-fairness Trade-offs for Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26369)
*Shubham Singh,Ian A. Kash,Mesrob I. Ohannessian*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文证明了在排序任务中，传统的评分函数（scoring functions）在同时优化效用（utility）和公平性（fairness）时存在本质缺陷，无法实现所有可能的效用-公平性权衡，而半贪婪后处理方法能够接近最优的权衡效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着算法公平性研究的兴起，现有方法倾向于通过学习评分函数来同时权衡效用和公平性。然而，评分函数是否足以实现所有效用-公平性权衡尚不明确，本文旨在揭示评分函数在这一目标上的局限性。

Method: 通过一系列反例，在通用的公平性公式下，从确定性评分函数、随机化评分函数、单查询和多查询公平性度量等多个维度证明评分函数的次优性。并采用半贪婪后处理方法在实证中展示更好的权衡效果。

Result: 评分函数在实现效用-公平性权衡时是次优的，该问题在确定性/随机化评分函数以及单查询/多查询公平性度量下均存在。半贪婪后处理方法能够以可计算的方式逼近穷举后处理的理想权衡效果。

Conclusion: 在追求效用与公平性权衡的任务中，不应仅依赖评分函数，后处理方法（特别是半贪婪策略）是更有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Scoring functions are used to represent the relevance of individual documents. In modern information retrieval or recommendation systems, they are often learned from data and play a pivotal role in ranking sets of documents or items in a way that maximizes utility to a query or user. With the recent interest in algorithmic fairness, the success of scoring has naturally led to methods that learn scores that simultaneously trade off fairness and utility. In this work, we show that in stark contrast with utility-centric objectives, scoring is sub-optimal in achieving all utility-fairness trade-offs. We establish this with a series of counter-examples with a generic fairness formulation. We show that the issue persists whether we have a deterministic scoring function or a randomized one, or whether we measure fairness at the scope of a single query or across multiple queries. On the positive side, we empirically demonstrate that semi-greedy post-processing has the potential to achieve much better trade-offs, often approaching the ideal of exhaustive post-processing in a tractable way.

</details>


### [119] [TileMaxSim: IO-Aware GPU MaxSim Scoring with Dimension Tiling and Fused Product Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26439)
*Ashutosh Sharma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对ColBERT等多向量检索模型中的MaxSim评分在GPU上存在的严重带宽瓶颈（仅达到峰值HBM带宽的5-18%），提出了TileMaxSim——一组IO感知的Triton内核。通过多查询SRAM分块、维度分块和融合乘积量化评分三大技术，TileMaxSim在NVIDIA H100上达到了80.2%的峰值HBM带宽，实现每秒8200万文档的评分速度，比朴素实现快220倍，同时保持精确的检索质量。作为ColBERTv2/PLAID的即插即用替代，它将100K候选集的评分延迟从268ms降至1.2ms（降低98%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量检索模型（如ColBERT）虽然通过细粒度token级MaxSim评分实现了最先进的检索精度，但现有的GPU实现存在严重的带宽利用率低下问题：朴素实现仅达到峰值HBM带宽的5-18%，原因是它们物化了Nq x Nd的相似度矩阵，浪费了大量内存流量。这严重限制了检索系统的吞吐量和延迟，尤其在大规模文档评分场景下。

Method: 提出TileMaxSim，一组IO感知的Triton内核，包含三项关键技术：(1) 多查询SRAM分块——将文档向量流经共享内存，同时在寄存器中累积每个查询token的最大值，每个嵌入向量仅从HBM读取一次；(2) 维度分块——将嵌入维度划分为128宽的块，支持d>128的嵌入评分；(3) 融合乘积量化评分——通过共享内存查找表，将HBM I/O减少高达31倍。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100 GPU上，TileMaxSim达到80.2%的峰值HBM带宽，每秒评分8200万文档（MS MARCO真实段落上为7160万/秒），比基于循环的评分加速220倍，比融合PyTorch加速6.5倍，比torch.compile加速6.6-8.5倍，比WARP的CPU引擎加速469倍。在ColBERTv2/PLAID中，将100K候选集的评分延迟从268ms降至1.2ms（降低98%）。在MS MARCO和三个BEIR基准测试上保持精确的检索质量（排名与参考MaxSim一致）。支持维度64-768、FP16/BF16/FP32，并支持数据并行多GPU分片。

Conclusion: TileMaxSim通过IO感知的内核设计，有效解决了MaxSim评分在GPU上的带宽瓶颈，实现了接近理论峰值的硬件利用率。该方法作为即插即用组件，在不牺牲检索质量的前提下，显著提升了多向量检索模型的推理效率。与同期工作相比，本文在维度分块（d>128）和融合乘积量化评分方面具有独特性。

Abstract: Multi-vector retrieval models such as ColBERT achieve state-of-the-art accuracy through fine-grained token-level MaxSim scoring, yet existing GPU implementations leave most hardware performance unused. We give a roofline analysis of MaxSim on modern GPUs and identify a severe bandwidth gap: naive implementations reach only 5-18% of peak HBM bandwidth because they materialize the Nq x Nd similarity matrix, wasting memory traffic on data that is consumed once and discarded. We present TileMaxSim, a family of IO-aware Triton kernels that close this gap via (1) multi-query SRAM tiling that streams document embeddings through shared memory while accumulating per-query-token maxima in registers, reading each embedding from HBM exactly once; (2) dimension tiling that partitions the embedding dimension into 128-wide chunks, enabling scoring for d > 128 embeddings that overflow shared memory; and (3) fused product-quantization scoring via shared-memory lookup tables, cutting HBM I/O by up to ~31x. On NVIDIA H100 GPUs, TileMaxSim reaches 80.2% of peak HBM bandwidth and scores 82M documents/second (71.6M/s on real MS MARCO passages), a 220x speedup over loop-based scoring, 6.5x over fused PyTorch, 6.6-8.5x over torch.compile, and 469x the scoring throughput of WARP's CPU engine on the same node. TileMaxSim preserves exact retrieval quality: on MS MARCO and three BEIR benchmarks, rankings match reference MaxSim. As a drop-in replacement in ColBERTv2/PLAID, it cuts scoring latency at 100K candidates from 268 ms to 1.2 ms (98% lower end-to-end latency). We further show constant throughput from 100K to 500K documents, data-parallel multi-GPU sharding, robustness across dimensions 64-768, and FP16/BF16/FP32 support. Concurrent work independently develops an IO-aware fused MaxSim kernel; we differ in dimension tiling for d > 128 and fused product-quantization scoring.

</details>


### [120] [GPUSparse: GPU-Accelerated Learned Sparse Retrieval with Parallel Inverted Indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26441)
*Ashutosh Sharma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GPUSparse是一个利用GPU加速的精确学习型稀疏检索系统，通过GPU并行倒排索引、批量scatter-add打分算法和融合Triton内核，在MS MARCO数据集上实现与CPU精确打分一致的结果（MRR@10=0.383），同时提供高达235x的加速（1.27ms vs 298ms每查询），并分析了工作效率与带宽利用率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型稀疏检索模型（如SPLADE）在推理时仍依赖CPU上的倒排索引遍历算法（如WAND），这成为大规模实时服务的基本瓶颈，需要GPU加速来解决。

Method: (1) GPU并行倒排索引，采用块对齐、warp合并的倒排列表；(2) 批量scatter-add打分算法，可同时处理数百个查询；(3) 融合Triton内核，分析工作效率与硬件利用率之间的权衡。

Result: 在MS MARCO段落排序（880万段落）上：MRR@10=0.383（与CPU一致），Recall@1000>=0.999，比Pyserini CPU加速235倍（1.27ms vs 298ms），吞吐量达787 QPS（批大小500），文档并行内核达到H100峰值HBM带宽的62.6%。

Conclusion: GPUSparse实现了GPU上的精确学习型稀疏检索，在不牺牲召回率的前提下获得了数量级的加速，揭示了GPU稀疏检索中工作效率与带宽效率之间的根本性权衡。

Abstract: Learned sparse retrieval models such as SPLADE achieve retrieval quality competitive with dense models while preserving the interpretability and exact-match advantages of sparse representations. However, inference-time scoring still relies on CPU-bound inverted index traversal algorithms (WAND, Block-Max WAND), creating a fundamental bottleneck for real-time serving at scale. We present GPUSparse, a system for GPU-accelerated exact learned sparse retrieval that introduces: (1) a GPU-parallel inverted index with block-aligned, warp-coalesced posting lists; (2) a batched scatter-add scoring algorithm that processes hundreds of queries simultaneously; and (3) fused Triton kernels with an analysis of the tradeoff between work-efficiency and hardware utilization. On MS MARCO passage ranking (8.8M passages) with real SPLADE embeddings, GPUSparse matches CPU exact scoring to three decimals (MRR@10=0.383, equal to Pyserini SPLADE at this precision; Recall@1000>=0.999 vs. dense matmul, the residual from floating-point tie-breaking) while providing a 235x speedup over Pyserini CPU at 8.8M documents (1.27ms vs. 298ms per query). Compared to Seismic (the fastest CPU sparse retrieval system), which trades 25% recall for speed (R@1000=0.738 vs. 0.983 exact), GPUSparse achieves exact scoring at 787 QPS throughput (batch 500) on the full 8.8M collection, with 1.3ms per query. Our document-parallel kernel reaches 62.6% of H100 peak HBM bandwidth, revealing a fundamental work-efficiency vs. bandwidth-efficiency tradeoff in GPU sparse retrieval. The reformulation of sparse scoring as scatter-add over an inverted index is shared with SPARe's iterative mode; our contribution is its fused-kernel realization, which we measure to be 23-270x faster than a faithful SPARe iterative reimplementation.

</details>


### [121] [Attributed, But Not Incremental: Cannibalization-Corrected Attribution for Large-Scale Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26690)
*Donghui Li,Bowen Yuan,Zili Yang,Qinxin Chen,Lijing Song*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对大规模付费获客系统中归因数据高估增量效果的问题，提出实验校准的归因修正框架，利用增量实验作为因果锚点，将稀疏提升测量转化为每日修正估计，并在业务层级间分配校准后的蚕食量。在TikTok全球市场部署后，蚕食率降低约15个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 付费归因（如DNU）在付费渠道与自然需求、品牌流量或其他获客渠道重叠时，会系统性高估真实的增量增长，导致归因-蚕食不匹配，扭曲增量ROI测量和大规模预算决策。

Method: 提出实验校准的归因修正框架：（1）以增量实验作为因果锚点，将稀疏的lift测量转化为每日修正估计；（2）在结构性一致性约束下，将校准后的蚕食量跨业务层级分配，使修正信号在生产粒度上可操作。

Result: 离线前向验证显示该框架相比原始归因和细粒度ML基线显著降低了校准误差。在TikTok全球多个市场部署后，蚕食率降低约15个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验校准的归因修正框架能有效解决大规模广告系统中归因高估增量的问题，提升增量ROI测量的准确性，并支持实际的预算和流量策略调整。

Abstract: In large-scale paid acquisition and growth advertising systems, production attribution outputs are widely used for daily budget allocation and channel diagnosis. However, paid-attributed conversions such as daily new users (DNU) may systematically overstate true incremental growth when paid channels overlap with organic demand, brand-driven traffic, or other acquisition channels. This attribution-cannibalization mismatch can distort incremental ROI measurement and budget decisions at scale.
  We propose an experiment-calibrated attribution correction framework that uses incrementality experiments as causal anchors to convert sparse lift measurements into daily correction estimates. To make the corrected signal actionable at production granularity, we further allocate calibrated cannibalization volume across business hierarchies under structural consistency constraints. Offline forward-in-time validation against channel-level incrementality experiment readouts shows that the proposed framework substantially reduces calibration error relative to raw attribution and fine-grained ML baselines. Deployed across multiple global TikTok markets, the system supported budget and traffic strategy adjustments that were followed by an approximately 15-percentage-point reduction in the measured cannibalization rate.

</details>


### [122] [A Shared IPTC Topic Space for Cross-Source Topic Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26845)
*Din Iskakov,Sebastian Gonçalves,Marco Idiat,Mendeli Vainstein,Aline Villavicencio,Ronaldo Menezes,Rodrigo Wilkens*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可复现的框架，通过IPTC媒体主题分类法将不同语料库映射到共享主题空间，实现跨来源的主题比较。该框架使用引导型BERTopic，在纽约时报2011语料库上经过多阶段筛选和校准，相比零样本基线方法在严格阈值下保持了更强的映射覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同媒体的主题注意力比较受到基础建模问题的阻碍：分别拟合的主题模型会产生无法直接对齐的语料库特定主题空间。

Method: 采用引导型BERTopic，通过加权关键词和目标质心与IPTC Media Topics（94个一级主题）评分，然后通过最大相似度规则向上聚合到17个IPTC父主题。框架经过多阶段开发筛选：广泛模型筛选、聚焦映射细化、严格终选比较、目标构建消融实验和阈值校准。

Result: 引导型家族在严格赋值阈值下比零样本基线保持更强映射覆盖率；父主题增强的目标构建提高了覆盖率和父主题一致性；覆盖率随阈值收紧逐渐下降而非骤降。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提供了一种外部锚定的方法，构建共享主题空间，实现可复现的跨来源主题比较。

Abstract: Comparing topic attention across different media is hindered by a fundamental modelling problem: topic models fitted separately to each corpus produce corpus-specific topic spaces that cannot be aligned directly. This paper presents a reproducible framework that places corpora in a single shared topic space defined by a taxonomy. Discovered topics are obtained with guided BERTopic, scored against the ninety-four IPTC Media Topics' taxonomy topics (level-1) through weighted keyword and target centroids, and then collapsed upward to seventeen IPTC parent topics by a maximum-similarity rule. The framework was developed and selected on a controlled New York Times 2011 corpus through a narrowing sequence: a broad model screen, a focused mapping refinement, a strict finalist comparison, a target-construction ablation, and a threshold calibration. In this corpus, the guided family retained substantially stronger mapped coverage than a zero-shot benchmark under stricter assignment thresholds, a parent-enriched target construction improved both coverage and parent consistency, and coverage declined gradually rather than collapsing as the assignment threshold was tightened. The contribution is an externally anchored method for constructing a shared topic space that enables reproducible cross-source topic comparison.

</details>


### [123] [TriPAH: Imbalance-Aware Tri-Prompt Affinity Hashing for Cross-Modal Medical Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27010)
*Jiaming Bian,Songming Li,Yurui Song,Yunfei Chen,Yichao Cao,Jun Long*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TriPAH框架，通过三提示亲和哈希、提示-令牌混合器、非对称多任务目标及患者级一致性模块，解决跨模态医学检索中的语义碎片化、长尾标签和量化不对齐问题，在三个数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态医学哈希检索方法存在三个关键问题：临床语言噪声导致语义碎片化、长尾标签分布、以及脆弱的量化过程削弱了模态间的语义对齐。

Method: 1) 本体引导的患者级三提示机制（Tri-Prompt）生成低噪声文本表示；2) 轻量级提示-令牌混合器实现分层多粒度对齐；3) 非对称多任务目标（多正例对比学习+不平衡感知分类+渐进量化正则化）；4) 患者级一致性模块稳定互补视图下的哈希码。

Result: 在三个公共医学数据集上，TriPAH显著优于所有当前最先进的跨模态哈希检索方法。

Conclusion: 通过提示工程与多任务联合优化的方式，能够有效缓解医学跨模态检索中的噪声与不平衡问题，实现更鲁棒的语义对齐和哈希编码。

Abstract: In the era of big medical data, efficient cross-modal retrieval is pivotal for evidence-based diagnosis and large-scale case management. Cross-modal medical hashing retrieval aims to enable efficient image-text search and support downstream tasks such as case-based reasoning and decision support by learning compact, semantically aligned binary codes. However, current methods suffer from semantic fragmentation due to noisy clinical language, long-tailed labels, and brittle quantization that weakens alignment. We propose TriPAH, a Tri-Prompt Affinity Hashing framework. TriPAH synthesizes ontology-grounded, patient-level prompts conditioned on normalized clinical cues to yield low-noise textual representations for initial alignment. A lightweight prompt-token mixer performs hierarchical, multi-granularity alignment and produces quantization-ready features under an asymmetric multi-task objective coupling multi-positive contrastive alignment, imbalance-aware classification, and progressive quantization regularization. A patient-level consistency module further stabilizes codes across complementary views. Extensive experiments on three public datasets demonstrate that TriPAH significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [124] [UniFormer: Efficient and Unified Model-Centric Scaling for Industrial Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27058)
*Bo Chen,Jinlong Jiao,Tijian Hu,Ruihao Zhang,Yanzhi Liu,Chenghou Jin,Qinglin Jia,Baixuan He,Hechang Pan,Yiwu Liu,Jian Liang,Chaoyi Ma,Ruiming Tang,Han Li,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出UniFormer，一个面向工业推荐系统的统一模型中心缩放框架，通过将建模空间分解为特征空间和任务空间、引入语义分词方案实现用户-物品解耦、多序列交叉注意力捕获异质行为模式、以及多视图FFN支持灵活参数缩放，在快手和快手极速版两个生产场景的在线A/B测试中显著提升了用户参与度和交互指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工业推荐系统主要采用组件中心模型缩放（如HyFormer、OneTrans），但局限于特征空间内进行跨模块协同缩放，缺乏统一的模型中心缩放框架来覆盖整体建模空间。

Method: 1) 将整体建模空间分解为特征空间和任务空间，分别由堆叠的特征空间交互模块和任务空间交互模块建模；2) 提出基于语义的分词方案实现用户-物品解耦，支持请求级推理加速；3) 采用多序列交叉注意力分别捕获异质行为模式，再通过自注意力增强交互建模；4) 引入多视图FFN支持不同建模组件间的灵活可扩展参数缩放。

Result: 在快手和快手极速版两个生产场景中，App停留时长分别提升+0.101%/+0.260%，观看时长分别提升+0.729%/+1.113%。

Conclusion: UniFormer作为一种高效统一的模型中心缩放框架，通过解耦特征与任务空间、语义分词加速推理、多序列交叉注意力防止偏好坍塌、多视图FFN支持参数扩展，显著优于现有方法，并已在工业场景中验证其有效性。

Abstract: Recently, substantial progress has been made in industrial recommendation through component-centric model scaling, where individual components such as behavior modeling, feature interaction, or task modeling are independently scaled to improve model capacity. Although recent methods such as HyFormer and OneTrans further explore cross-module co-scaling by jointly modeling behavior and interaction, their designs are still confined to the feature space and lack a unified model-centric scaling framework over the overall modeling space. In this paper, we propose UniFormer, an efficient and unified model-centric scaling framework for industrial recommender systems. To improve efficiency, UniFormer decomposes the overall modeling space into feature and task spaces, which are modeled by stacked Feature-space Interaction Modules and Task-space Interaction Modules, respectively. Moreover, UniFormer introduces semantic-based tokenization scheme to enable user-item decoupling, thereby achieving request-level inference acceleration. To prevent preference collapse, UniFormer employs multi-sequence cross-attention to separately capture heterogeneous behavior patterns, followed by the self-attention to enhance interaction modeling. Besides, dedicated multi-view FFNs are introduced to support flexible and scalable parameter scaling across different modeling components. Extensive online A/B testing in two production scenarios, Kuaishou and Kuaishou Lite, shows that UniFormer consistently improves user engagement and interaction metrics, achieving gains of +0.101%/+0.260% in App Stay Time and +0.729%/+1.113% in Watch Time, respectively.

</details>


### [125] [TRUST: Item-Calibrated Interval Evidence for Temporal Session-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27214)
*Linjiang Guo,Nitin Bisht,Shiqing Wu,Yifan Yin,Guandong Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TRUST提出了一种基于物品自身时间间隔分布校准的会话推荐框架，通过相对化时间间隔提升推荐性能，并在多个数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于时间的会话推荐方法假设相同的时间间隔在不同物品上携带相似的兴趣信号，但实证研究发现这一假设不成立——每个物品有其自身的间隔分布，因此时间间隔应相对于所属物品进行解释。

Method: 提出TRUST框架，通过评分函数将观测到的时间间隔相对于对应物品的经验间隔分布进行校准，并利用该评分函数指导全局邻居采样、会话图编码和最终兴趣聚合三个模块。

Result: 在公开数据集上，TRUST consistently优于代表性时间与非时间基线方法；插件实验显示该评分函数可作为模型无关方法提升现有时间会话推荐器；消融实验表明在每个模块内校准时间信号（而非移除模块）能持续改进各模块性能。

Conclusion: 时间间隔应相对于物品自身的分布进行解释，TRUST通过相对化校准策略有效提升了会话推荐的准确性，且其核心评分函数具有良好的模型无关可迁移性。

Abstract: Temporal signals have been widely used in session-based recommendation to infer user interest. Existing temporal session-based recommenders primarily rely on absolute interval values, implicitly assuming that the same interval carries similar interest signals across items. However, we empirically find that this assumption does not hold: each item has its own interval distribution, so an interval should be interpreted relative to the item it belongs to. Based on this observation, we propose TRUST, a framework that evaluates each observed interval relative to the empirical interval distribution of the corresponding item. Specifically, we propose a score function to guide global neighbor sampling, session graph encoding, and final interest aggregation. Experiments on public datasets show that TRUST consistently improves over representative temporal and non-temporal baselines, and plug-in experiments further show that the proposed scoring function can improve existing temporal session recommenders as a model-agnostic method. Component-wise ablations further show that calibrating the temporal signals within each module, rather than removing the module itself, consistently improves neighbor sampling, session graph encoding, and interest aggregation.

</details>


### [126] [NOVA: A Verification-Aware Agent Harness for Architecture Evolution in Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27243)
*Shaohua Liu,Liang Fang,Yilong Sun,Shudong Huang,Qingsong Luo,Xiaoyang Chen,Dongqiang Liu,Chuangang Ma,Zhenzhen Chai,Henghuan Wang,Shijie Quan,Changyuan Cui,Zhangbin Zhu,Peng Chen,Wei Xu,Lei Xiao,Haijie Gu,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: NOVA是一个面向工业广告推荐系统的等级感知代理框架，通过架构梯度、验证级联和L1-L4任务控制，实现了架构演进的自动化验证与优化。在L2和L3任务上分别达到54.5%和60.0%的有效通过率，缩短文献到生产的周期超13倍，在线A/B测试中pCVR目标GMV提升1.25%-2.02%，pCVR偏差降低37.3%-66.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业广告推荐模型的架构演进依赖专家经验，难以规模化；现有AutoML仅能调优超参数，通用LLM编码代理生成的代码虽可运行但可能导致静默失败，缺乏对推荐架构有效性的验证机制。

Method: 提出NOVA框架：1）架构梯度：受SGD启发的非可微更新信号，聚合历史修改、验证诊断、指标反馈和轨迹记忆；2）验证级联：依次检查结构语义、局部可执行性、离线有效性和在线影响，阻断无效候选并记录失败模式；3）L1-L4任务级控制：匹配自动化程度与任务复杂度和风险，高风险任务交由Copilot人工监督。

Result: 在工业广告系统中，L2 ScaleUp任务有效通过率54.5%，L3 Literature-to-Production任务有效通过率60.0%，静默失败率低于编码代理基线；文献到生产周期人力投入时间缩短超13倍；在线A/B测试中三个pCVR目标的GMV分别提升+1.25%、+1.70%、+2.02%，pCVR偏差降低58.8%、66.7%、37.3%。

Conclusion: NOVA通过验证感知的自动化架构演进框架，有效解决了工业推荐系统架构升级中可扩展性差和静默失败问题，实现了显著的业务指标提升和人力效率改进。

Abstract: Industrial advertising recommender models are continuously improved through architecture evolution. Upgrades such as RankMixer, TokenMixer-Large, and MixFormer show that better structures remain a key source of quality and business gains. Yet developing such upgrades in production is expert-intensive and difficult to scale. Existing automation is insufficient: AutoML mainly tunes hyper-parameters, while effective gains often require cross-module changes under strict constraints; generic LLM coding agents optimize for runnable code, but runnable code does not imply a valid recommender architecture. Candidates may pass local tests while causing silent failures that degrade performance.
  We present NOVA, a level-aware agent harness for verification-aware architecture evolution. NOVA uses an architecture gradient, an SGD-inspired, non-differentiable update signal that aggregates prior modifications, verification diagnostics, metric feedback, and trajectory memory to guide the next modification. A verification cascade checks structure semantics, local executability, offline effectiveness, and online impact; invalid candidates are blocked early, with failure patterns recorded as forbidden directions. L1--L4 task-level control matches automation to task complexity and risk, routing high-risk tasks to Copilot for human oversight. Deployed in an industrial advertising system, NOVA achieves the highest effective pass rate on L2 ScaleUp and L3 Literature-to-Production tasks (54.5% and 60.0%), reduces silent failures compared with coding-agent baselines, and shortens one literature-to-production cycle by over 13x in human-attended time. In online A/B testing, the selected L3 candidate improves GMV on three pCVR objectives by +1.25%, +1.70%, and +2.02%, while reducing pCVR bias by 58.8%, 66.7%, and 37.3%.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Detecting and Controlling Sycophancy with Cascading Linear Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26155)
*Maty Bohacek,Rishub Jain,Nicholas Dufour,Thomas Leung,Chris Bregler,Roma Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种迭代数据生成管道，通过分离随行为线性缩放的特征（而非简单二元对比样本），来更好地解耦和识别模型中的级联线性特征。在检测和引导模型远离谄媚行为（sycophancy）的任务上，该方法发现了线性可分的子空间，在检测、确定性评分和稳健引导方面匹配或超越基线方法，同时计算成本更低、可解释性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的激活引导方法依赖大量对比样本对来明确展示期望/非期望行为，但这些简单二元对难以充分解耦模型内部负责特定行为的特征，限制了可解释性框架的可靠性和引导效果。

Method: 提出一种迭代数据生成管道，通过构建展示行为线性缩放程度的级联样本（而非简单二元对比），来分离级联线性特征，从而更好地解耦与行为相关的特征子空间。

Result: 在谄媚行为检测和引导任务上，通过级联样本发现的谄媚特征形成线性可分的子空间，能选择出更清晰对应目标行为的模型激活。该方法在检测、确定性评分和稳健引导上匹配或超越LLM-as-a-judge和系统提示基线，同时计算需求更低、可解释性更强。

Conclusion: 超越简单二元对比样本，采用展示行为线性缩放特征的级联样本，能够更有效地解耦和识别模型中的线性特征，为更可靠、低成本的模型行为解释与引导提供了新路径。

Abstract: Interpreting and controlling model behaviors through activation steering methods requires many pairs of contrastive samples that clearly exhibit desired or undesired behavior. These data pairs determine the degree to which interpretability frameworks can reliably detect model features responsible for a behavior, and therefore the ability to steer models toward or away from such behavior. In this work, we present an iterative data generation pipeline that isolates cascading linear features responsible for a behavior. Specifically, we show how moving beyond simple binary pairs of samples, and instead isolating samples that show degrees of features that scale linearly with behavior, allows for better disentanglement of features. We focus on detecting and steering away from sycophancy -- the tendency of language models to prioritize user validation. We demonstrate that sycophancy features discovered through cascading samples form linearly separable subspaces, and allow for selection of model activations that more clearly correspond to the desired behavior than baseline approaches. We also evaluate their ability to enable detection, deterministic scoring, and robust steering, and see that they either match or outperform LLM-as-a-judge and system prompting baselines while providing lower computational demand and more interpretability guarantees. Code & Data: https://cascading-feats.github.io/

</details>


### [128] [Life After Benchmark Saturation: A Case Study of CORE-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26158)
*Nitya Nadgir,Sayash Kapoor,Kangheng Liu,Peter Kirgis,Matilda Orona,Stephan Rabanser,Tilman Bayer,Abhishek Shetty,Yue Ling,Derrick Chan-Sew,Rumi Nakagawa,Saiteja Utpala,Zachary S. Siegel,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出超越准确率的六维智能体评估框架，并以CORE-Bench Hard为案例证明即使准确率饱和，通过分析构造效度、泛化性、效率、可靠性、模型与脚手架相对重要性及人机协作增益，仍能获得有意义的性能洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试在准确率饱和后往往被直接替换，忽略了评估智能体性能的其他关键维度，导致评估范式过于单一，无法全面衡量智能体的真实能力。

Method: 以CORE-Bench Hard为案例，识别构造效度威胁并改进为CORE-Bench v1.1和CORE-Bench OOD；测量效率、可靠性、模型与脚手架性能；通过小规模随机实验测量人机协作速度提升。

Result: 准确率饱和后，CORE-Bench v1.1在效率、可靠性、模型性能和脚手架性能维度上仍能提供有用信息；人机协作比纯人工快约两倍，且该效应可能被低估。

Conclusion: 提出了一套超越准确率中心评估范式的更严格替代方案，强调多维度评估对于理解智能体性能的重要性。

Abstract: When a benchmark's accuracy saturates, it is often retired and replaced with a more challenging version. We show that this approach privileges accuracy and misses the opportunity to study six other key dimensions of agent performance: construct validity issues such as shortcuts, out-of-distribution generalizability, efficiency, reliability, the relative importance of the model versus the scaffold, and uplift from human-agent collaboration. We use CORE-Bench Hard, a benchmark for computational reproducibility of scientific code, as a case study to demonstrate that measuring agents along these dimensions yields meaningful insights into agent performance even after accuracy saturates. First, we surface threats to construct validity in CORE-Bench Hard that are difficult to anticipate with less capable agents. We introduce an improved benchmark, CORE-Bench v1.1, and an out-of-distribution task suite, CORE-Bench OOD. Second, we find that despite accuracy saturation, CORE-Bench v1.1 remains useful for measuring efficiency, reliability, model performance, and scaffold performance. Finally, we conduct a small-scale randomized experiment to measure uplift from human-agent collaboration on real-world computational reproducibility tasks. We find a statistically significant speedup by about a factor of two -- likely underestimated due to one-fifth of human-only reproductions reaching the time limit before completing -- and describe various other findings. Together, our contributions present a more rigorous alternative to the dominant accuracy-centric evaluation paradigm.

</details>


### [129] [Refusal Lives Downstream of Persona in Chat Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26161)
*Viola Zhong,Qirui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现指令微调模型中的拒绝机制与人格特质（尤其是顺从性）存在交互：顺从人格会“门控”拒绝行为。通过在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct中进行方向干预实验，证实顺从人格方向激活会抑制拒绝（Llama拒绝率从97%降至2%），且拒绝方向仅在后期层部分恢复拒绝效果。拒绝的最终表达依赖于人格方向的参与，不能孤立看待。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究分别识别了拒绝方向（refusal direction）和人格特质方向（persona direction），但两者作为独立机制被研究，未考虑其交互作用。本文旨在揭示顺从人格如何门控拒绝表达，从而更完整地理解模型行为控制机制。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct中提取顺从人格方向（compliant model-persona direction）和拒绝方向（refusal direction），通过激活干预（steering）和投影移除（projecting out）等方法，在不同层进行干预实验，观察对拒绝率的影响。

Result: 顺从人格方向激活显著抑制拒绝（Llama拒绝率从97%降至2%）；拒绝方向仅在后期层能部分恢复拒绝行为；在后期层窗口移除人格方向可恢复拒绝基线水平，而移除随机方向则无效。

Conclusion: 拒绝机制并非孤立方向，其最终表达（expression stage）在后期层被人格方向门控；拒绝的计算（computation）先于表达阶段，表达阶段依赖人格参与。将拒绝视为单一隔离方向会忽略其对人格的依赖。

Abstract: Linear directions in activation space have been identified for both refusal and persona traits in instruction-tuned chat models, but the two have been studied as separate mechanisms. We show they interact: a compliant persona gates refusal. In Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we extract a compliant model-persona direction and a refusal direction and intervene on both. Compliant persona steering suppresses refusal -- in Llama, the refusal rate falls from 97% to 2%. Reintroducing the refusal direction partially restores refusal at late layers but not at early ones. Projecting out the persona direction in a late-layer window restores it to baseline; projecting out a random direction does not. Refusal is therefore gated at the late-layer expression stage, downstream of where it is computed. Treating refusal as a single isolated direction misses its dependence on persona.

</details>


### [130] [AlgoEvolve: LLM-driven Meta-evolution of Algorithmic Trading Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26173)
*Dhruv Sharma,Gautam Shroff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AlgoEvolve框架，利用大语言模型作为语义变异算子，通过进化算法自动生成、评估和迭代改进可执行的算法交易策略，并引入元进化外循环优化提示词，在噪声、非平稳的金融环境中实现了自适应交易策略的涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的语义进化方法主要应用于静态编程基准测试，尚未在算法交易这一具有噪声、非平稳、高度不连续特性的复杂动态领域中得到验证，作者旨在探索LLM语义进化在该领域的可行性。

Method: 提出AlgoEvolve框架，包含两个循环：内循环使用LLM作为语义变异算子对交易策略（Python代码）进行进化生成与评估；外循环采用元进化方式优化内循环的提示词，自动发现更好的搜索启发式策略，平衡探索与利用并减少零交易失败。

Result: 系统表现出涌现的自适应策略逻辑，能够自主切换交易规则；元进化外循环发现的启发式策略在平衡探索与利用、减少零交易失败方面持续优于初始人工设计的提示词。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的语义进化方法为复杂环境中的持续程序合成提供了一种可行的途径，在算法交易领域展现出良好的适应性和有效性。

Abstract: Recent work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can act as semantic mutation operators for the evolutionary discovery of programs and proofs. Most current applications focus on static coding benchmarks. We extend this paradigm to algorithmic trading. This domain is uniquely challenging because it is noisy, non-stationary, and highly discontinuous. We present AlgoEvolve, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework that generates, evaluates, and iteratively improves executable trading strategies. These strategies are expressed as Python code and evaluated through a rigorous testing protocol. Across multiple experiments, the system exhibits emergent regime-adaptive strategy logic, including autonomous shifts in trading rules. We further introduce a meta-evolutionary outer loop that evolves the prompts guiding program synthesis in the inner loop. This outer loop discovers improved search heuristics. These heuristics balance exploration and exploitation while reducing zero-trade failures. They consistently outperform initial human-designed instructions. The results demonstrate that LLM-based semantic evolution provides a viable approach for continual program synthesis in complex environments.

</details>


### [131] [Agentic Analysis for Agentic Infrastructure: An LLM-Powered Pipeline for Comparative Governance of DAO and Corporate AI Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26203)
*Yutian Wang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于LLM的比较分析流程，用于大规模治理话语分析，通过对ERC-8004（无许可、链上）和Google A2A（企业主导）两种智能体互操作性标准的治理参与记录进行分析，发现治理形式影响主题关注点，但两种体制在参与不平等和社区碎片化程度上相当，无许可环境下话语对齐更密集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI智能体协议日益增多，但塑造其互操作性标准的治理结构在经验层面仍缺乏系统研究，作者希望通过大规模实证分析填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了LLM驱动的比较分析流程，集成自动标注、神经主题建模和多层网络分析，对4,323条治理参与记录进行分析，比较ERC-8004（无许可链上）和Google A2A（企业主导）两种标准。

Result: 治理形式影响主题关注点；两种体制在参与不平等和社区碎片化程度上水平相当；无许可环境中话语对齐更密集，表明开放治理可能促进更大的主题趋同。

Conclusion: LLM辅助方法能够推进技术治理的实证研究，对设计更公平的智能体AI标准具有启示意义。

Abstract: As AI agent protocols proliferate, the governance structures shaping their interoperability standards remain empirically underexamined. We introduce an LLM-powered comparative pipeline for large-scale governance discourse analysis, integrating automated annotation, neural topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to study socio-technical power structures at scale. We validate it on two contrasting standards for agent interoperability: ERC-8004 (permissionless, on-chain) and Google A2A (corporate-led). Analyzing 4,323 governance participation records, we combine LLM-assisted coding, topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to examine how institutional design shapes thematic priorities and community structure. We find that while governance form influences substantive focus, both regimes exhibit comparable levels of participation inequality and community fragmentation. Discourse alignment is denser in the permissionless setting, suggesting that open governance may foster greater thematic convergence despite decentralized participation. These findings illustrate how LLM-assisted methods can advance the empirical study of technology governance, with implications for designing more equitable agentic AI standards. All data and code are openly available.

</details>


### [132] [Knowledge-augmented Agentic AI for Mental Health Medication Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26205)
*Huizi Yu,Jian Liu,Wenkong Wang,Lingyao Li,Jiayan Zhou,Zhaoqian Xue,Xiang Li,Xinxin Lin,Zhiying Liang,Zhuoru Wu,Siyuan Ma,Xin Ma,Lizhou Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于知识图谱的多智能体框架，整合了来自Reddit、WebMD和FDA的不良事件报告系统共约52.7万条记录，涵盖九种抗抑郁药。通过大语言模型实体识别管道（F1最高0.973），发现患者社区数据与FDA报告存在部分独立的安全信号，且社区中许多不良事件出现时间早于FDA记录（例如舍曲林提前数百天）。该框架基于ATC-N、ICD-10和MedDRA词汇体系构建Neo4j知识图谱，保留数据来源，区分监管事实与患者经验，为更可审计的精神科药物信息提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者越来越多地在线上寻求药物信息，但精神科药物的安全性知识分散在权威但抽象的监管不良事件记录和有经验但未经验证的患者叙述中。在精神科领域，缺乏上下文的信息可能加剧恐惧、反安慰剂效应和药物不依从性，因此需要一种既能整合两类数据又不混淆证据与轶事的方案。

Method: 构建了一个基于知识图谱的多智能体框架，整合466,525条Reddit帖子、60,782条WebMD评论和20年FDA不良事件报告系统数据（涵盖九种抗抑郁药）。使用大语言模型实体识别管道进行药物和疾病实体抽取，与医生标注进行基准比较。基于ATC-N、ICD-10和MedDRA标准词汇体系构建Neo4j知识图谱，保留数据来源和可追溯性。

Result: 实体识别管道在药物和疾病识别上F1最高分别达0.969和0.973。两个社区平台（Reddit和WebMD）之间的一致性远高于它们与FDA报告的一致性（Jaccard相似度最高0.905）。以舍曲林为例，许多不良事件在社区来源中出现时间比FDA记录早数百天。

Conclusion: 患者生成数据构成了部分独立的药物安全信号，来源感知的整合方法能建立更可审计的精神科药物信息体系，其实用性和对患者的益处有待前瞻性验证。

Abstract: Patients increasingly seek medication information online, yet safety knowledge for psychiatric drugs is split between regulatory adverse-event records, which are authoritative but abstract, and patient narratives, which are experience-near but unvalidated. Integrating them without conflating evidence and anecdote is especially consequential in psychiatry, where poorly contextualised information can amplify fear, nocebo responses, and non-adherence. Here we develop a provenance-aware, knowledge-graph-based multi-agent framework unifying 466,525 Reddit posts, 60,782 WebMD reviews, and twenty years of U.S. FDA Adverse Event Reporting System records for nine antidepressants. A large-language-model entity-recognition pipeline benchmarked against physician annotations reached highest F1 scores of 0.969 for medications and 0.973 for conditions. The two community platforms were far more concordant with each other (overlap up to a Jaccard similarity of 0.905) than with regulatory reports, indicating that patient-generated data form a partly independent safety signal. For sertraline, many adverse events appeared in community sources hundreds of days before the corresponding FDA date. A Neo4j knowledge graph grounded in ATC-N, ICD-10, and MedDRA vocabularies preserves provenance, keeping every claim traceable and regulatory facts distinct from patient experience. These results establish source-aware integration as a route to more auditable psychiatric medication information, with usefulness and patient benefit to be tested prospectively.

</details>


### [133] [Accelerating Skill Assessment in Chess: A Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26267)
*Tianyuan Zhou,Zhizheng Fu,Tianming Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DD-Elo，一种融合漂移扩散模型（DDM）的象棋评分系统，通过整合棋步级别的数据来捕捉技能快速变化，在保持与传统Elo系统理论一致性的同时，显著提升了评分响应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Elo评分系统仅依赖比赛结果，存在固有的响应延迟问题，无法利用棋局过程中的细粒度质量信息。而将棋步级信息纳入评分调整面临噪声大、游戏状态空间巨大的挑战。

Method: 借鉴认知神经科学中的漂移扩散模型（DDM），将技能表达建模为决策过程，整合棋步级数据以捕捉快速技能波动。提供了严格的数学推导，证明DD-Elo与传统Elo系统的偏差有界。

Result: 大量实验表明DD-Elo在适应技能变化方面比Elo更快，具有更好的响应性。

Conclusion: DD-Elo为象棋评级生态系统提供了一种可解释、高响应性且向后兼容的解决方案。

Abstract: Rating systems such as Elo serve as the gold standard for matchmaking in competitive chess. However, they inherently suffer from response lag due to their exclusive reliance on match outcomes, neglecting the granular quality of gameplay. Nevertheless, incorporating move-by-move information into rating adjustments presents a significant challenge given the substantial noise and the vastness of the game-state space. To address this, we propose the Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System (DD-Elo), a novel skill assessment framework inspired by the drift diffusion model (DDM) from cognitive neuroscience. By modeling skill expression as a decision-making process, our model integrates move-level data to capture rapid skill fluctuations. We provide a rigorous mathematical derivation proving that DD-Elo maintains a bounded deviation from the traditional Elo system, ensuring theoretical alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DD-Elo adapts to skill changes faster than Elo. Our findings suggest that DD-Elo offers an explainable, highly responsive, and backward-compatible solution for chess rating ecosystems. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aquila-zhou1/DD-Elo .

</details>


### [134] [Governing Actions, Not Agents: Institutional Attestation as a Governance Model for Autonomous AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26298)
*Jakob Salfeld-Nebgen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算治理模型，要求AI代理在实施高风险不可逆行动（如临床处方或软件部署）时，必须获得独立权威来源的凭证认证，而非依赖对其推理过程的监控。该模型保留代理的规划与推理自主权，但高风险的执行权受确定性策略约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI代理可能执行不可逆的高风险行动（如临床处方、生产软件部署），现有方法依赖监控代理推理过程，但人类机构治理强大自主行动者时并非监控其推理，而是要求在行动节点提供独立认证的证据。作者希望将这种制度模式形式化为AI代理系统的计算治理模型。

Method: 提出计算治理模型：代理保留规划与推理的完全自主权，但对指定的高风险行动无执行权限；执行需满足前提条件，每个条件由独立权威来源认证，声明意图经密码学绑定，由确定性策略评估；决策记录在防篡改日志中，可供独立复核。提供了概念验证实现，并以软件部署和临床处方为例进行说明。

Result: 通过概念验证实现展示了模型可行性；以软件部署和临床处方两个高风险场景为例，说明了模型如何在实际应用中发挥作用。

Conclusion: 该治理模型通过分离自主推理与受控执行，在保留AI代理灵活性的同时，为高风险行动提供了可验证的安全保障机制，借鉴了人类社会中有效的治理模式。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents may begin to perform consequential, irreversible actions such as clinical prescribing and production software deployment. This paper observes that human institutions have governed powerful autonomous actors not by monitoring their reasoning but by requiring independently attested evidence at the point of consequential action. We formalise this institutional pattern as a computational governance model for AI agent systems. Under the proposed model, an agent retains full autonomy over planning and reasoning but holds no execution authority over designated high-risk actions. Execution is conditional on preconditions that are each independently attested by a separate authoritative source, cryptographically bound to a declared intent, and evaluated by a deterministic policy. Decisions are recorded in a tamper-evident log amenable to independent re-verification. We present a proof-of-concept implementation and illustrate the model with examples from software deployment and clinical prescribing.

</details>


### [135] [COrigami: An AI Pipeline for Co-Designing Flat-Foldable Visually Recognisable Origami](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26299)
*Tom Zahavy,Shaobo Hou,Thomas Tumiel,James Doran,Francesco Faccio,Xidong Feng,Alex Havrilla,Igor Khytryi,Chenglei Li,Lisa Schut,Vivek Veeriah,Arijan Abrashi,Michał Kosmulski,Robert J. Lang,Nick Robinson,Brandon Wong,Marcus Chiam,Gloria Fang,Satinder Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了COrigami，一个端到端的AI驱动流水线，能够根据自然语言描述生成满足数学折叠约束和主观审美的折纸折痕图案，通过结合语义骨架生成、基础布局计算、可折叠图案求解、形状塑造以及基于强化学习的自主审美评估循环，实现了多目标物理约束下的AI协同创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在可验证解的问题上取得了显著成功，但在生成同时满足严格几何约束和主观视觉美感的物理艺术作品方面仍面临挑战。本文旨在解决计算折纸这一数学刚性环境中的设计难题。

Method: 提出了COrigami端到端AI流水线，包含五个阶段：语义骨架生成、基础布局计算、可折叠折痕图案求解、折叠图案形状塑造、以及基于强化学习的自主审美评估循环驱动的模型优化。

Result: 该系统可作为高效的协作助手，生成结构化的初始设计点，供人类艺术家进一步扩展和塑造，实现了满足多目标物理约束的可靠、基于数学的协同创造力。

Conclusion: 通过将算法优化与自主审美批评相结合，展示了AI系统如何满足多目标物理约束，从而实现可靠的、基于数学的协同创造性。

Abstract: While generative AI has achieved remarkable success in solving problems with verifiable solutions, generating physical art that satisfies both strict geometric constraints and subjective visual aesthetics remains a challenge. This paper presents an approach to tackle these difficulties in the domain of computational origami, a mathematically rigid environment that grounds artistic design within the equations of flat foldability. We present COrigami, an end-to-end AI-driven pipeline that assists the design cycle by generating crease patterns from natural language. Our pipeline involves generating a semantic stick figure, computing a base packing, solving for a flat-foldable crease pattern, shaping the flat-folded crease pattern, and refining the generated model using reinforcement learning driven by an autonomous aesthetic evaluation loop. Our system acts as a highly effective collaborative assistant, generating structural starting points that human artists can further expand and shape. By integrating algorithmic optimisation with autonomous aesthetic critique, this work demonstrates how AI systems can satisfy multi-objective physical constraints to enable reliable, mathematically grounded co-creativity.

</details>


### [136] [The Verification Horizon: No Silver Bullet for Coding Agent Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26300)
*Binghai Wang,Chenlong Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Jiajun Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Mouxiang Chen,Rongyao Fang,Siyuan Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuheng Jing,Zeyao Ma,Zeyu Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，随着编码智能体能力增强，验证（而非生成）已成为更难的问题。验证面临意图本质上的欠指定性和优化加剧代理与意图差距的双重困难。作者从可扩展性、忠实性、鲁棒性三个维度刻画验证信号质量，研究了四种奖励构造方法，并通过实验证明验证需与生成器协同进化，固定奖励函数无法持续有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直觉认为验证比生成容易，但对于当今的编码智能体，这一直觉正在被颠覆：生成复杂候选解已不再困难，而可靠地验证它们反而成为更难的问题。每个我们能构建的验证器都只是人类意图的代理，而非意图本身，这使得验证面临双重困难。

Method: 从可扩展性、忠实性、鲁棒性三个维度刻画验证信号质量；研究四种奖励构造：通用编码任务的测试验证器、前端任务的评分标准验证器、真实世界智能体任务中以用户为验证器、长周期任务的自动化智能体验证器；在不同任务类型和策略能力水平下进行深入分析和实验。

Result: 针对性验证设计能有效抑制奖励破解，提升任务完成质量，在多个内部和公开基准上取得显著提升。没有任何固定奖励函数能在策略能力持续增长时保持有效；验证必须与生成器协同进化。

Conclusion: 验证必须与生成器协同进化，固定奖励函数无法随策略能力增长而持续有效。

Abstract: A classical intuition holds that verifying a solution is easier than producing one. For today's coding agents, this intuition is being inverted: as foundation models develop stronger reasoning capabilities and engineering harnesses grow more sophisticated, generating complex candidate solutions is no longer difficult -- reliably verifying them has become the harder problem. Every verifier we can build is only a proxy for human intent, never the intent itself. This makes verification subject to a twofold difficulty: first, intent is underspecified by nature, making it inherently hard to faithfully check whether it has been fulfilled; second, during model training, optimization widens the gap between proxy and intent -- manifesting as reward hacking or signal saturation. To address this, we characterize the quality of verification signals along three dimensions -- scalability, faithfulness, and robustness -- and argue that achieving all three simultaneously is the central challenge. We further study four reward constructions: a test verifier for general coding tasks, a rubric verifier for frontend tasks, the user as verifier for real-world agent tasks, and an automated agent verifier for long-horizon tasks. Across different task types and policy capability levels, we conduct in-depth analysis and experiments on the core challenges of reward design and how to more effectively leverage reward signals. Experiments show that targeted verification design can effectively suppress reward hacking, improve task completion quality, and achieve significant gains across multiple internal and public benchmarks. These experiences collectively point to a core observation: no fixed reward function can remain effective as policy capability continues to grow; and verification must co-evolve with the generator.

</details>


### [137] [How Do Tool-Augmented LLM Agents Perform on Real-World Energy Analytics Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26346)
*David Akinpelu,Akintonde Abbas,Rereloluwa Alimi,Ayodeji Lana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对能源领域缺乏动态、多步推理的智能体基准评估这一空白，构建了包含243个专家级问题的能源市场分析评估环境，设计了包含实时API、监管检索、数据库等工具套件的LLM智能体评估框架，并对开源和闭源模型进行了多维度比较分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用及专业领域（金融、编程、法律等）的智能体基准已广泛存在，但能源领域的评估仍局限于静态知识回忆。能源行业需要实时数据检索、专业监管和市场知识以及多步定量推理能力，现有评估无法反映这些真实需求，存在关键空白。

Method: 构建了243个专家级问题（涵盖市场数据检索分析、知识检索解释、高级定量建模决策三类），配置了包括实时电力市场API、监管文档检索、公用事业费率数据库、资产优化模型及RAG等工具的套件，采用多维评估协议（方法正确性、答案准确度、属性对齐、来源有效性）进行打分。

Result: 对闭源和开源LLM进行了比较分析，揭示了模型能力与领域工具在高风险专业场景中的交互作用，并公开了关键工件以支持可复现性。

Conclusion: 填补了能源领域缺乏动态、多步推理智能体基准的空白，为能源市场分析中LLM智能体的能力评估提供了标准化框架，并推动了该方向的可复现研究。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks have emerged across general-purpose and domain-specific settings, including finance, coding, law, and drug discovery, yet energy-domain evaluations remain largely limited to static knowledge recall. This is a critical gap for a sector that requires live data retrieval, specialized regulatory and market knowledge, and multi-step quantitative reasoning under real-world constraints.
  We present an empirical study of tool-augmented LLM agents on real-world energy market analytics tasks. Our evaluation environment includes 243 expert-curated problems across three categories: (1) Market Data Retrieval and Analysis, (2) Knowledge Retrieval and Interpretation, and (3) Advanced Quantitative Modeling and Decision Analytics. Tasks include price and demand analysis, tariff impact modeling, asset revenue and returns estimation, hedging strategy analysis, and optimization modeling, with problems spanning multiple difficulty levels.
  Agents are equipped with a configurable suite of domain tools, including live electricity market APIs for major U.S. ISOs, regulatory docket search, utility tariff databases, asset optimization models, and retrieval-augmented generation over energy market documents. We assess agent responses using a multi-dimensional evaluation protocol that scores approach correctness, answer accuracy, attribute alignment, and source validity, with category-aware routing to match scoring criteria to question type. We evaluate both closed-source and open-source LLMs, providing a comparative analysis of how model capability and domain tooling interact in a high-stakes professional domain. Key artifacts are publicly released to support reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [138] [Instruction Bleed: Cross-Module Interference in Prompt-Composed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26356)
*Ching-Yu Lin,Yifan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了提示组合型智能体系统中的组合行为泄漏（CBL）问题，即编辑一个提示模块会无声地改变其他模块的行为，即使它们之间没有共享变量或可执行依赖。通过一个部署的工作评估智能体（Claude Sonnet 4.6，144次试验）和一个三通道协议，发现仅内容通道产生可检测的配对效应（Cohen's d = 0.63），但未导致推荐翻转——这是一种亚阈值干扰，在标准QA中不可见，但在数千次决策中累积。CBL与已知的智能体故障正交，论文提供了操作定义、可复用协议、可证伪预测集和系统级特征描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示组合型智能体系统存在一个反复出现的故障模式：编辑一个提示模块会无声地改变其他模块的行为，但缺乏形式化定义和系统测量方法。

Method: 通过一个144次试验的工作评估智能体（Claude Sonnet 4.6），使用三通道协议（体积、内容、形式）扰动非焦点模块，测量跨模块干扰效应。

Result: 仅内容通道产生可检测的配对效应（Cohen's d = 0.63，bootstrap 95% CI排除零），但未导致推荐翻转，表明是一种亚阈值干扰模式。

Conclusion: CBL是独立于已知智能体故障的新故障轴，需要将跨模块干扰测量纳入提示组合型智能体评估的要求中。

Abstract: Practitioners of prompt-composed agentic systems report a recurring failure mode: editing one prompt module silently shifts the behavior of others despite no shared variable or executable dependency. We formalize this as compositional behavioral leakage (CBL): interference between modules sharing a context window. CBL is enabled by architectural non-isolation: transformer self-attention provides no formal boundary between concatenated modules. We probe CBL on a deployed job-evaluation agent (Claude Sonnet 4.6, 144 trials) through a reusable three-channel protocol that perturbs non-focal modules along volume, content, and form. Only the content channel produces a detectable paired effect (Cohen's d = 0.63, bootstrap 95% CI excluding zero); no recommendation flipped -- a sub-threshold regime invisible to standard QA but compounding across the thousands of decisions a deployed agent makes. CBL is orthogonal to known agent-failure axes (adversarial injection, cognitive degradation, multi-agent fault propagation, privacy leakage). We contribute an operational definition, a reusable protocol, a falsifiable prediction set, and a system-class characterization, establishing cross-module interference measurement as a requirement for prompt-composed agent evaluation.

</details>


### [139] [What We are Missing in Multimodal LLM Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26348)
*Po-han Li,Shenghui Chen,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）评估存在滞后，现有基准局限于孤立任务，未能有效衡量跨模态信息整合能力。本文梳理了评估现状与基准分类，识别出时间-空间连贯性、物理世界理解、多模态一致性和选择性注意力等关键评估缺口，填补这些缺口对衡量多模态智能的真实进展至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs能力快速提升，但评估方法发展严重滞后，现有基准多限于孤立任务，无法揭示模型是否真正实现了跨模态信息整合。

Method: 通过审视当前MLLMs评估手段，综述现有基准分类体系，识别评估中的关键缺口（时间-空间连贯性、物理世界理解、多模态一致性、选择性注意力）。

Result: 指出了当前评估体系的四大关键缺失维度：时间-空间连贯性、物理世界理解、多模态一致性、选择性注意力。

Conclusion: 填补上述评估缺口对于衡量多模态智能的真实进展和揭示能力边界至关重要。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can process diverse inputs, e.g., text, images, audio, and video, and generate textual responses. While their capabilities have advanced rapidly, evaluation of such models has not kept pace. Most existing evaluation benchmarks are limited to isolated tasks and reveal little about whether a model integrates information across modalities. We examine current means for evaluating MLLMs and review the existing benchmark taxonomy to identify gaps, including temporal-spatial coherence, physical world understanding, multimodal consistency, and selective attention. Addressing these gaps is essential for measuring real progress in multimodal intelligence and exposing capability boundaries.

</details>


### [140] [AgentX: Towards Agent-Driven Self-Iteration of Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26859)
*Changxin Lao,Fei Pan,Guozhuang Ma,Han Li,Huihuang Lin,Jijun Shi,Kangzhi Zhao,Kun Gai,Mo Zhou,Qinqin Zhou,Quan Chen,Ruochen Yang,Shifu Bie,Shuang Yang,Shuo Yang,Wenhao Li,Wentao Xie,Xiao Lv,Xuming Wang,Yijun Wang,Yiming Chen,Yusheng Huang,Zhongyuan Wang,Zibo Zhao,Zijie Zhuang,Baoning Xia,Chao Liu,Chaoyi Ma,Chubo He,Dawei Cong,Feng Jiang,Gang Wang,Guilin Xia,Hanwen Xu,Jiahong Xie,Jiahui Qiao,Jian Liang,Jiangfan Yue,Jing Wang,Jinghan Yang,Jinghui Jia,Kan Qin,Lei Wang,Ming Li,Peilin Song,Pengbo Xu,Qiang Luo,Ruiming Tang,Shiyang Liu,Shuxian Jin,Tao Wang,Tao Zhang,Xiang Gao,Xianghan Li,Yingsong Luo,Yiwen Ning,Yongcheng Liu,Yuan Guo,Zhaojie Liu,Zhenkai Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentX是一个在生产环境中部署的多智能体系统，能够自主进行推荐算法的假设生成、代码实现、在线实验评估和自我进化，将推荐算法迭代从人工密集型过程转变为工业化、可规模化的闭环系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推荐算法迭代存在结构性瓶颈：从想法到上线完全依赖人类工程师完成假设生成、代码修改、A/B实验和结果归因，导致创新速度与人力规模线性相关，无法随着计算资源、证据积累和实验知识增长而实现复合增长。

Method: AgentX采用四阶段闭环多智能体架构：(1) Brainstorm Agent综合历史实验、系统架构、数据分析和外部研究生成可执行提案；(2) Developing Agent基于仓库感知的代码生成和多维可靠性验证将提案转化为生产代码；(3) Evaluation Agent通过有护栏否决权的A/B实验进行安全在线评估；(4) Harness Evolution层（SGPO）将执行轨迹蒸馏为语义梯度更新，持续优化智能体自身能力。

Result: AgentX已在生产环境中部署，能够以人工工作流无法维持的规模和速度自主进行推荐实验的生成、实施、评估和学习。

Conclusion: AgentX通过将推荐算法迭代重构为自进化的闭环系统，打破了人力瓶颈，实现了创新速度与实验知识、计算资源的复合增长，使系统从简单的自动化迈向自我持续改进。

Abstract: Recommendation algorithm iteration is moving from an artisanal, engineer-bound process toward an industrialized research loop, but this transition remains blocked by a structural execution bottleneck: the idea-to-launch cycle still depends on human engineers to generate hypotheses, modify production code, launch A/B experiments, and attribute online results. Innovation therefore scales linearly with headcount rather than compounding with evidence, compute, and accumulated experimental knowledge. We present AgentX, a production-deployed multi-agent system that fundamentally restructures this production function. AgentX operates as a self-evolving development engine: it autonomously generates, implements, evaluates, and learns from recommendation experiments at a scale and pace that no manual workflow can sustain.
  The system orchestrates four tightly coupled stages in a closed loop. A Brainstorm Agent synthesizes evidence from historical experiments, system architecture, data analysis, and external research into ranked, executable proposals. A Developing Agent translates each proposal into production-ready code through repository-grounded generation and multi-dimensional reliability verification. An Evaluation Agent conducts safe online rollout with guardrail-vetoed A/B judgment, converting both successes and failures into structured knowledge assets. A Harness Evolution layer (SGPO) then distills execution trajectories into semantic-gradient updates that continuously sharpen the agents themselves -- making the system not merely automated, but self-improving.

</details>


### [141] [OpenFinGym: A Verifiable Multi-Task Gym Environment for Evaluating Quant Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26350)
*Kaicheng Zhang,Wen Ge,Lei Jiang,Weixin Yang,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Jialin Yu,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenFinGym 是一个统一的量化金融智能体开发环境，覆盖预测、市场生成、实时交易和欺诈检测等多个任务，提供自动化任务构建流水线、容器化运行时、回测引擎等功能，支持SFT和RL后训练集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体在量化金融领域的评估分散在孤立任务上，缺乏对金融工作流多阶段、相互依赖特性的考虑，导致高估智能体能力并无法揭示其在泛化、真实市场交互和金融意义决策上的弱点。

Method: OpenFinGym 包含：(1) 统一执行和验证接口覆盖预测、市场生成、实时交易和欺诈检测；(2) 自动化任务构建流水线，将量化金融论文转化为可执行任务包；(3) 容器化运行时与主机端验证器服务，支持可扩展智能体部署并防止训练-测试泄漏；(4) 低延迟数据流设计的回测引擎；(5) 长期和事件市场预测的延迟决议支持；(6) SFT和RL后训练集成。

Result: 论文提出了OpenFinGym平台本身，实验验证结果未在摘要中明确给出。

Conclusion: OpenFinGym通过统一的多任务环境、自动化任务构建和容器化部署，为量化金融智能体提供了更全面、更真实的评估框架，有望弥补现有评估体系的碎片化问题。

Abstract: Although large language model agents are increasingly applied to quantitative-finance workflows, their evaluation remains fragmented across isolated tasks, while the financial relevance of benchmark tasks is often overlooked. Yet financial workflows are inherently multi-stage, spanning interdependent tasks such as forecasting, strategy construction, risk management, and trading.
